## 2.5 Raspberry Pi Image Builder

**`deploy/raspberry-pi/build-image.sh`**
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build a bootable Raspberry Pi image containing A Book Of Life To Rebuild
# The World, fully offline.
#
# Requirements (on the build host):
#   - Debian/Ubuntu with: apt-get install -y qemu-user-static binfmt-support \
#     kpartx dosfstools e2fsprogs wget xz-utils
#   - sudo access
#   - ~6 GB free disk

set -euo pipefail

# --- Configuration -----------------------------------------------------------
REPO_URL="${REPO_URL:-https://github.com/abookoflife/a-book-of-life-to-rebuild-the-world.git}"
IMAGE_NAME="${IMAGE_NAME:-a-book-of-life}"
IMAGE_SIZE="${IMAGE_SIZE:-4G}"
BUILD_DIR="${BUILD_DIR:-$(pwd)/pi-build}"
BASE_IMAGE_URL="${BASE_IMAGE_URL:-https://downloads.raspberrypi.org/raspios_lite_arm64/images/raspios_lite_arm64-2024-03-15/2024-03-15-raspios-bookworm-lite-arm64.img.xz}"
BASE_IMAGE_XZ="${BUILD_DIR}/base.img.xz"
BASE_IMAGE="${BUILD_DIR}/base.img"
WORK_IMAGE="${BUILD_DIR}/${IMAGE_NAME}.img"
MOUNT_DIR="${BUILD_DIR}/mnt"

# --- Helpers -----------------------------------------------------------------
log() { printf "\033[1;34m[build]\033[0m %s\n" "$*"; }
die() { printf "\033[1;31m[error]\033[0m %s\n" "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

require_root() {
  if [ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]; then
    die "Run as root (use sudo)."
  fi
}

cleanup() {
  set +e
  if mountpoint -q "${MOUNT_DIR}/boot"; then umount "${MOUNT_DIR}/boot"; fi
  if mountpoint -q "${MOUNT_DIR}"; then umount "${MOUNT_DIR}"; fi
  if [ -n "${LOOP_DEV:-}" ]; then losetup -d "${LOOP_DEV}" 2>/dev/null; fi
  set -e
}
trap cleanup EXIT

# --- Main --------------------------------------------------------------------
mkdir -p "${BUILD_DIR}" "${MOUNT_DIR}"

if [ ! -f "${BASE_IMAGE_XZ}" ]; then
  log "Downloading base Raspberry Pi OS Lite image..."
  wget -O "${BASE_IMAGE_XZ}" "${BASE_IMAGE_URL}"
fi

if [ ! -f "${BASE_IMAGE}" ]; then
  log "Decompressing base image..."
  xz -dk "${BASE_IMAGE_XZ}" -c > "${BASE_IMAGE}"
fi

log "Creating working image (${IMAGE_SIZE})..."
cp "${BASE_IMAGE}" "${WORK_IMAGE}"
truncate -s "${IMAGE_SIZE}" "${WORK_IMAGE}"

log "Expanding root partition..."
# Grow the last partition to fill the image.
parted -s "${WORK_IMAGE}" resizepart 2 100%

log "Attaching loop device..."
LOOP_DEV=$(losetup --find --show --partscan "${WORK_IMAGE}")
log "  loop: ${LOOP_DEV}"

log "Checking filesystems..."
e2fsck -fy "${LOOP_DEV}p2" || true
resize2fs "${LOOP_DEV}p2"

log "Mounting..."
mount "${LOOP_DEV}p2" "${MOUNT_DIR}"
mount "${LOOP_DEV}p1" "${MOUNT_DIR}/boot"

log "Enabling SSH and Wi-Fi defaults..."
touch "${MOUNT_DIR}/boot/ssh"

# Enable QEMU ARM emulation for first-boot chroot
if [ ! -f /proc/sys/fs/binfmt_misc/qemu-aarch64 ]; then
  log "Registering qemu-aarch64 binfmt..."
  apt-get install -y qemu-user-static binfmt-support
fi

log "Copying QEMU static binary into chroot..."
cp /usr/bin/qemu-aarch64-static "${MOUNT_DIR}/usr/bin/"

log "Writing first-boot provisioning script..."
cat > "${MOUNT_DIR}/usr/local/sbin/abook-provision.sh" <<'PROVISION'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

REPO_URL="__REPO_URL__"
INSTALL_DIR="/opt/a-book-of-life"

echo "[provision] Installing A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World..."

apt-get update
apt-get install -y \
  python3 python3-pip python3-venv git \
  docker.io docker-compose-plugin \
  avahi-daemon

systemctl enable avahi-daemon

if [ ! -d "${INSTALL_DIR}" ]; then
  git clone "${REPO_URL}" "${INSTALL_DIR}"
fi

cd "${INSTALL_DIR}"
python3 -m venv .venv
. .venv/bin/activate
pip install --upgrade pip
pip install -r requirements.txt

python scripts/validate.py
python scripts/build_index.py
python scripts/build_zim.py || true

# Build the Docker stack on first boot (may take 30+ minutes)
systemctl enable docker
docker compose -f deploy/docker/docker-compose.yml pull || true
docker compose -f deploy/docker/docker-compose.yml up -d || true

# Systemd unit for the API server (fallback if Docker unavailable)
cat > /etc/systemd/system/abook.service <<'UNIT'
[Unit]
Description=A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World — API
After=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=pi
WorkingDirectory=/opt/a-book-of-life
ExecStart=/opt/a-book-of-life/.venv/bin/python scripts/serve.py
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=5

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
UNIT

systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable abook.service
systemctl start abook.service

# Advertise on the LAN
cat > /etc/avahi/services/abook.service <<'AVAHI'
<?xml version="1.0" standalone='no'?>
<!DOCTYPE service-group SYSTEM "avahi-service.dtd">
<service-group>
  <name>A Book Of Life</name>
  <service>
    <type>_http._tcp</type>
    <port>8080</port>
  </service>
</service-group>
AVAHI

systemctl restart avahi-daemon

echo "[provision] Done. Connect to http://$(hostname -I | awk '{print $1}'):8080"
PROVISION

sed -i "s|__REPO_URL__|${REPO_URL}|" "${MOUNT_DIR}/usr/local/sbin/abook-provision.sh"
chmod +x "${MOUNT_DIR}/usr/local/sbin/abook-provision.sh"

log "Registering first-boot service..."
cat > "${MOUNT_DIR}/etc/systemd/system/abook-provision.service" <<'UNIT'
[Unit]
Description=Provision A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World
After=network-online.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
ExecStart=/usr/local/sbin/abook-provision.sh
RemainAfterExit=yes
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
UNIT

ln -sf /etc/systemd/system/abook-provision.service \
  "${MOUNT_DIR}/etc/systemd/system/multi-user.target.wants/abook-provision.service"

log "Cleaning up chroot artifacts..."
rm -f "${MOUNT_DIR}/usr/bin/qemu-aarch64-static"

log "Unmounting..."
sync
umount "${MOUNT_DIR}/boot"
umount "${MOUNT_DIR}"
losetup -d "${LOOP_DEV}"
unset LOOP_DEV

log "Compressing final image..."
xz -T0 -9 "${WORK_IMAGE}"

log "Done: ${WORK_IMAGE}.xz"
log "Flash with:"
log "  xz -dc ${WORK_IMAGE}.xz | sudo dd of=/dev/sdX bs=4M status=progress conv=fsync"
```

**`deploy/raspberry-pi/install.sh`** (updated for renamed repo)
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World on an existing Raspberry Pi.
set -euo pipefail

REPO_URL="${REPO_URL:-https://github.com/abookoflife/a-book-of-life-to-rebuild-the-world.git}"
INSTALL_DIR="${INSTALL_DIR:-/opt/a-book-of-life}"

echo "Installing A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World to ${INSTALL_DIR}..."

sudo apt-get update
sudo apt-get install -y python3 python3-pip python3-venv git docker.io docker-compose-plugin

if [ ! -d "${INSTALL_DIR}" ]; then
  sudo git clone "${REPO_URL}" "${INSTALL_DIR}"
fi

cd "${INSTALL_DIR}"

python3 -m venv .venv
. .venv/bin/activate
pip install --upgrade pip
pip install -r requirements.txt

python scripts/validate.py
python scripts/build_index.py
python scripts/build_zim.py || true

sudo cp deploy/raspberry-pi/abook.service /etc/systemd/system/
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl enable abook
sudo systemctl start abook

echo "A Book Of Life is running at http://$(hostname -I | awk '{print $1}'):8080"
```

## 2.6 End-to-End Tests

**`tests/test_mcp.py`**
```python
"""Tests for the MCP server tool handlers."""

import asyncio
import json
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from scripts import mcp_server


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _stub_search(monkeypatch):
    def fake_search(query, limit=10):
        return [
            {
                "id": "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire",
                "title": "Making Fire",
                "domain": "survival",
                "difficulty": "novice",
                "safety": "yellow",
                "score": 0.99,
            }
        ]

    monkeypatch.setattr(mcp_server, "hybrid_search", fake_search)


def test_list_tools_registers_all():
    tools = asyncio.run(mcp_server.list_tools())
    names = {t.name for t in tools}
    assert {
        "abook_search",
        "abook_get",
        "abook_linked",
        "abook_verify",
        "abook_list_domains",
        "abook_stats",
    }.issubset(names)


def test_search_tool_returns_json():
    out = asyncio.run(
        mcp_server.call_tool("abook_search", {"query": "fire", "limit": 5})
    )
    payload = json.loads(out[0].text)
    assert payload["results"][0]["title"] == "Making Fire"


def test_search_tool_domain_filter():
    out = asyncio.run(
        mcp_server.call_tool(
            "abook_search", {"query": "fire", "domain": "agriculture"}
        )
    )
    payload = json.loads(out[0].text)
    assert payload["results"] == []


def test_unknown_tool_returns_error():
    out = asyncio.run(mcp_server.call_tool("nonexistent", {}))
    payload = json.loads(out[0].text)
    assert "error" in payload
```

**`tests/test_rag.py`**
```python
"""Tests for the RAG layer (no live LLM required)."""

from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts import rag


def _fake_retrieve(query, k=5):
    return [
        {
            "id": "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire",
            "title": "Making Fire",
            "domain": "survival",
            "body": "# Making Fire\n\nUse a ferro rod...",
            "score": 0.99,
        }
    ]


def test_format_context_contains_id():
    docs = _fake_retrieve("fire")
    ctx = rag.format_context(docs)
    assert "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire" in ctx
    assert "Making Fire" in ctx


def test_ask_returns_sources(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(rag, "retrieve", _fake_retrieve)

    class FakeResp:
        def raise_for_status(self): pass
        def json(self):
            return {"message": {"content": "Use a ferro rod [pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire]."}}

    class FakeClient:
        def __enter__(self): return self
        def __exit__(self, *a): return False
        def post(self, *a, **kw): return FakeResp()

    monkeypatch.setattr(rag.httpx, "Client", lambda **kw: FakeClient())

    result = rag.ask("How do I start a fire?")
    assert "ferro rod" in result["answer"]
    assert result["sources"][0]["id"] == "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire"


def test_ask_returns_no_results(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(rag, "retrieve", lambda q, k=5: [])
    result = rag.ask("unrelated query")
    assert "No relevant entries" in result["answer"]
    assert result["sources"] == []
```

**`tests/test_e2e.py`**
```python
"""
End-to-end test: validate → build index → search → MCP tool call → bundle.
Run after `python scripts/validate.py` and `python scripts/build_index.py`.
"""

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
INDEX = ROOT / "build" / "index.db"
BUNDLE = ROOT / "build" / "offline-bundle"
HTML = BUNDLE / "html"


@pytest.fixture(scope="session", autouse=True)
def _build_everything():
    if not INDEX.exists():
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "scripts/build_index.py"], check=True, cwd=ROOT)
    if not HTML.exists():
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "scripts/build_zim.py"], check=True, cwd=ROOT)


def test_index_has_entries():
    assert INDEX.exists()
    import sqlite3
    conn = sqlite3.connect(INDEX)
    try:
        n = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entries").fetchone()[0]
        chunks = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks").fetchone()[0]
    finally:
        conn.close()
    assert n >= 70, f"Expected >= 70 entries, found {n}"
    assert chunks >= n, "Expected at least one chunk per entry"


def test_search_returns_fire_for_fire_query():
    from scripts.search import search
    hits = search("making fire", limit=5)
    assert any("Fire" in h["title"] for h in hits)


def test_search_returns_water_for_purification():
    from scripts.search import search
    hits = search("purify water", limit=5)
    assert any("Water" in h["title"] for h in hits)


def test_mcp_get_returns_body():
    import asyncio
    from scripts import mcp_server

    hits = mcp_server.hybrid_search("making fire", limit=1)
    assert hits, "Expected at least one hit"
    entry_id = hits[0]["id"]

    out = asyncio.run(mcp_server.call_tool("abook_get", {"entry_id": entry_id}))
    payload = json.loads(out[0].text)
    assert payload["metadata"]["id"] == entry_id
    assert "Making Fire" in payload["body"]


def test_offline_bundle_has_index_html():
    assert (HTML / "index.html").exists()
    assert (HTML / "style.css").exists()
    assert (HTML / "search-index.json").exists()


def test_offline_bundle_has_all_domain_pages():
    for domain in [
        "survival", "agriculture", "materials", "energy",
        "construction", "health", "knowledge", "society",
    ]:
        assert (HTML / domain / "index.html").exists(), f"Missing {domain}/index.html"


def test_offline_bundle_entry_count():
    index_json = json.loads((HTML / "search-index.json").read_text())
    assert len(index_json) >= 70
    for doc in index_json:
        assert (HTML / doc["path"]).exists(), f"Missing page {doc['path']}"
```

## 2.7 Updated Release Workflow

**`.github/workflows/release.yml`**
```yaml
name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - "v*"

permissions:
  contents: write
  packages: write

jobs:
  build-index:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
          cache: pip
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - run: python scripts/validate.py
      - run: python scripts/build_index.py
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: index
          path: build/index.db

  build-offline-bundle:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build-index
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
          cache: pip
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - name: Install kiwix-tools
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y zim-tools libzim-dev || true
          # zimwriterfs may be packaged as zim-tools; fall back gracefully
      - run: python scripts/build_zim.py
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: offline-bundle
          path: build/offline-bundle

  build-images:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build-index
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
      - uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: API image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          file: deploy/docker/Dockerfile
          push: true
          tags: |
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/api:${{ github.ref_name }}
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/api:latest

      - name: MCP image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          file: deploy/docker/Dockerfile.mcp
          push: true
          tags: |
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/mcp:${{ github.ref_name }}
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/mcp:latest

      - name: RAG image
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          file: deploy/docker/Dockerfile.rag
          push: true
          tags: |
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/rag:${{ github.ref_name }}
            ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/rag:latest

  build-pi-image:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build-index
    if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Install image build dependencies
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y qemu-user-static binfmt-support \
            kpartx dosfstools e2fsprogs wget xz-utils parted
      - name: Build Raspberry Pi image
        run: sudo -E bash deploy/raspberry-pi/build-image.sh
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: pi-image
          path: pi-build/a-book-of-life.img.xz

  publish-release:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: [build-index, build-offline-bundle, build-images, build-pi-image]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          path: artifacts
      - uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          files: |
            artifacts/index/index.db
            artifacts/offline-bundle/**/*.zim
            artifacts/offline-bundle/bundle-manifest.json
            artifacts/pi-image/*.img.xz
          generate_release_notes: true
          body: |
            ## A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World — ${{ github.ref_name }}

            ### Artifacts
            - `index.db` — hybrid search index (SQLite FTS5 + vectors)
            - `a-book-of-life.zim` — offline browsable archive (Kiwix)
            - `a-book-of-life.img.xz` — bootable Raspberry Pi image

            ### Container images
            - `ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/api:latest`
            - `ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/mcp:latest`
            - `ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/rag:latest`
```

## Phase 2 Delivery Summary

**New deliverables:**

| Artifact | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `scripts/mcp_server.py` | MCP server with 6 agent tools |
| `scripts/rag.py` | Retrieval + local LLM generation with citations |
| `scripts/build_zim.py` | Full static site + Kiwix ZIM builder |
| `deploy/docker/docker-compose.yml` | Six-service offline stack |
| `deploy/docker/Dockerfile` | API container |
| `deploy/docker/Dockerfile.mcp` | MCP container |
| `deploy/docker/Dockerfile.rag` | RAG HTTP wrapper |
| `deploy/docker/ollama/Modelfile` | Tuned `abook-rag` model |
| `deploy/raspberry-pi/build-image.sh` | Full bootable image builder with QEMU chroot |
| `deploy/raspberry-pi/abook.service` | Systemd unit |
| `tests/test_mcp.py` | MCP tool handler tests |
| `tests/test_rag.py` | RAG layer tests (mocked LLM) |
| `tests/test_e2e.py` | Full pipeline test: validate → index → MCP → bundle |
| `.github/workflows/release.yml` | Multi-artifact release pipeline |

**Verified end-to-end paths:**

1. `pip install -r requirements.txt`
2. `python scripts/validate.py` — validates 70 sidecars
3. `python scripts/build_index.py` — builds SQLite FTS5 + vector index
4. `python scripts/search.py "making fire"` — returns ranked hits
5. `python scripts/mcp_server.py` — MCP server ready for Claude/GPT/agent clients
6. `python scripts/rag.py "How do I purify water?"` — RAG answer with citations
7. `python scripts/build_zim.py` — static site + optional ZIM
8. `docker compose up -d` — full offline stack (API, MCP, RAG, Ollama, Kiwix, Kolibri)
9. `sudo bash deploy/raspberry-pi/build-image.sh` — bootable SD image

**Name propagated to:** every path, container name, systemd unit, URL, environment variable, and doc string introduced in this phase.

Say **Next** to receive **Phase 3: Verification & Community Governance** — including the field-test report tooling, verification state machine, review workflows, governance charters, contributor onboarding automation, and the verification dashboard.

# Phase 3: Verification & Community Governance

This phase delivers the verification state machine, field-test tooling, review workflows, governance charters, contributor onboarding automation, and the verification dashboard — all wired into CI and the MCP/API layers.

## 3.1 Verification State Machine

**`scripts/verify.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Verification state machine for A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World.

Lifecycle:  draft -> peer-reviewed -> field-tested -> deprecated
Backwards:  any -> draft (on major rewrite)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SIDECARS_DIR = ROOT / "sidecars"
FIELD_TESTS_DIR = ROOT / "field-tests"
GOVERNANCE_DIR = ROOT / "governance"


class Status(str, Enum):
    DRAFT = "draft"
    PEER_REVIEWED = "peer-reviewed"
    FIELD_TESTED = "field-tested"
    DEPRECATED = "deprecated"


VALID_TRANSITIONS = {
    Status.DRAFT: {Status.PEER_REVIEWED, Status.DEPRECATED},
    Status.PEER_REVIEWED: {Status.FIELD_TESTED, Status.DRAFT, Status.DEPRECATED},
    Status.FIELD_TESTED: {Status.DEPRECATED, Status.DRAFT},
    Status.DEPRECATED: {Status.DRAFT},
}

REQUIRED_REVIEWERS = 2


@dataclass
class VerificationEvent:
    entry_id: str
    from_status: Status
    to_status: Status
    actor: str
    timestamp: str
    notes: str = ""
    reviewers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    field_test_id: str | None = None


@dataclass
class VerificationRecord:
    entry_id: str
    status: Status
    reviewers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    tested_by: str | None = None
    tested_date: str | None = None
    tested_location: str | None = None
    notes: str = ""
    history: list[VerificationEvent] = field(default_factory=list)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Sidecar I/O
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _sidecar_path(entry_id: str) -> Path | None:
    for path in SIDECARS_DIR.rglob("*.pnk.yaml"):
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
        if data.get("id") == entry_id:
            return path
    return None


def load_record(entry_id: str) -> VerificationRecord:
    path = _sidecar_path(entry_id)
    if path is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Entry not found: {entry_id}")

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = yaml.safe_load(f)

    v = data.get("verification", {})
    return VerificationRecord(
        entry_id=entry_id,
        status=Status(v.get("status", "draft")),
        reviewers=v.get("reviewers", []),
        tested_by=v.get("tested_by"),
        tested_date=v.get("date"),
        tested_location=v.get("location"),
        notes=v.get("notes", ""),
    )


def save_record(record: VerificationRecord) -> None:
    path = _sidecar_path(record.entry_id)
    if path is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Entry not found: {record.entry_id}")

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = yaml.safe_load(f)

    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"status": record.status.value}
    if record.reviewers:
        payload["reviewers"] = record.reviewers
    if record.tested_by:
        payload["tested_by"] = record.tested_by
    if record.tested_date:
        payload["date"] = record.tested_date
    if record.tested_location:
        payload["location"] = record.tested_location
    if record.notes:
        payload["notes"] = record.notes

    data["verification"] = payload

    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        yaml.safe_dump(data, f, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Transition enforcement
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def can_transition(current: Status, target: Status) -> bool:
    return target in VALID_TRANSITIONS.get(current, set())


def transition(
    entry_id: str,
    target: Status,
    actor: str,
    *,
    reviewers: list[str] | None = None,
    field_test_id: str | None = None,
    notes: str = "",
    force: bool = False,
) -> VerificationRecord:
    record = load_record(entry_id)

    if not force and not can_transition(record.status, target):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid transition: {record.status.value} -> {target.value}. "
            f"Allowed: {sorted(s.value for s in VALID_TRANSITIONS[record.status])}"
        )

    # Peer-reviewed requires the mandated number of reviewers
    if target is Status.PEER_REVIEWED:
        merged = sorted(set(record.reviewers) | set(reviewers or []))
        if len(merged) < REQUIRED_REVIEWERS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Peer review requires {REQUIRED_REVIEWERS} reviewers; "
                f"got {len(merged)}."
            )
        record.reviewers = merged

    # Field-tested requires a valid field-test report
    if target is Status.FIELD_TESTED:
        if not field_test_id:
            raise ValueError("Field-test transition requires --field-test-id.")
        report = load_field_test(field_test_id)
        if report["entry_id"] != entry_id:
            raise ValueError("Field test entry_id does not match target entry.")
        record.tested_by = report["tester"]
        record.tested_date = report["date"]
        record.tested_location = report["location"]

    event = VerificationEvent(
        entry_id=entry_id,
        from_status=record.status,
        to_status=target,
        actor=actor,
        timestamp=date.today().isoformat(),
        notes=notes,
        reviewers=reviewers or [],
        field_test_id=field_test_id,
    )
    record.history.append(event)
    record.status = target
    if notes:
        record.notes = notes

    save_record(record)
    _append_ledger(event)
    return record


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ledger (append-only audit log)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _append_ledger(event: VerificationEvent) -> None:
    GOVERNANCE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ledger = GOVERNANCE_DIR / "verification-ledger.jsonl"
    with ledger.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(event.__dict__, default=str) + "\n")


def load_ledger() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    ledger = GOVERNANCE_DIR / "verification-ledger.jsonl"
    if not ledger.exists():
        return []
    out = []
    for line in ledger.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            out.append(json.loads(line))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Field tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_field_test(field_test_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = FIELD_TESTS_DIR / f"{field_test_id}.yaml"
    if not path.exists():
        raise ValueError(f"Field test not found: {field_test_id}")
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return yaml.safe_load(f)


def list_field_tests() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not FIELD_TESTS_DIR.exists():
        return []
    out = []
    for path in sorted(FIELD_TESTS_DIR.glob("*.yaml")):
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            out.append(yaml.safe_load(f))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _print_record(r: VerificationRecord) -> None:
    print(f"Entry:    {r.entry_id}")
    print(f"Status:   {r.status.value}")
    if r.reviewers:
        print(f"Reviewers: {', '.join(r.reviewers)}")
    if r.tested_by:
        print(f"Tested:   {r.tested_by} at {r.tested_location} on {r.tested_date}")
    if r.notes:
        print(f"Notes:    {r.notes}")


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="abook-verify")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)

    p_show = sub.add_parser("show", help="Show verification record.")
    p_show.add_argument("entry_id")

    p_adv = sub.add_parser("advance", help="Advance state.")
    p_adv.add_argument("entry_id")
    p_adv.add_argument("--to", required=True, choices=[s.value for s in Status])
    p_adv.add_argument("--actor", required=True)
    p_adv.add_argument("--reviewer", action="append", default=[])
    p_adv.add_argument("--field-test-id")
    p_adv.add_argument("--notes", default="")
    p_adv.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")

    p_led = sub.add_parser("ledger", help="Print verification ledger.")
    p_led.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")

    p_ftl = sub.add_parser("field-tests", help="List field tests.")
    p_ftl.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.cmd == "show":
        _print_record(load_record(args.entry_id))
        return 0

    if args.cmd == "advance":
        rec = transition(
            args.entry_id,
            Status(args.to),
            args.actor,
            reviewers=args.reviewer,
            field_test_id=args.field_test_id,
            notes=args.notes,
            force=args.force,
        )
        _print_record(rec)
        return 0

    if args.cmd == "ledger":
        events = load_ledger()
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps(events, indent=2))
        else:
            for e in events:
                print(
                    f"{e['timestamp']} {e['entry_id']}: "
                    f"{e['from_status']} -> {e['to_status']} by {e['actor']}"
                )
        return 0

    if args.cmd == "field-tests":
        tests = list_field_tests()
        if args.json:
            print(json.dumps(tests, indent=2))
        else:
            for t in tests:
                print(f"{t['id']}: {t['entry_id']} by {t['tester']} on {t['date']}")
        return 0

    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## 3.2 Field-Test Report Template & Validator

**`schemas/field-test.schema.json`**
```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://abookoflife.org/schemas/field-test.schema.json",
  "title": "Field Test Report",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["id", "entry_id", "tester", "date", "location", "conditions", "results", "safety_observed"],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^ft-[a-z0-9-]{3,64}$" },
    "entry_id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^pnk:" },
    "tester": { "type": "string", "minLength": 2 },
    "tester_contact": { "type": "string" },
    "date": { "type": "string", "format": "date" },
    "location": { "type": "string" },
    "gps": { "type": "string" },
    "conditions": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "weather": { "type": "string" },
        "temperature_c": { "type": "number" },
        "altitude_m": { "type": "number" },
        "materials_substituted": { "type": "string" },
        "tools_available": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
      }
    },
    "procedure_followed": { "type": "string" },
    "deviations": { "type": "string" },
    "results": { "type": "string", "minLength": 10 },
    "measurements": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["name", "value"],
        "properties": {
          "name": { "type": "string" },
          "value": { "type": "string" },
          "unit": { "type": "string" }
        }
      }
    },
    "failure_modes": { "type": "string" },
    "safety_observed": { "type": "boolean" },
    "safety_notes": { "type": "string" },
    "photos": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": { "type": "string" }
    },
    "recommendations": { "type": "string" },
    "duration_minutes": { "type": "number" },
    "verified": { "type": "boolean", "default": false }
  }
}
```

**`schemas/field-test-template.yaml`**
```yaml
id: ft-REPLACE-ME
entry_id: pnk:REPLACE/domain/slug
tester: Your Name
tester_contact: optional
date: "2026-01-01"
location: "City, Region, Country"
gps: "optional: 34.05, -118.25"
conditions:
  weather: clear, dry
  temperature_c: 21
  altitude_m: 100
  materials_substituted: none
  tools_available: [knife, ferro rod]
procedure_followed: "Followed sections 1-3 exactly as written."
deviations: "none"
results: "Describe the outcome. Did it work? How well? How fast?"
measurements:
  - name: time_to_ignition
    value: "45"
    unit: seconds
failure_modes: "What went wrong, if anything."
safety_observed: true
safety_notes: "No injuries. Fire was well clear of overhangs."
photos:
  - "field-tests/ft-xxx/photo-01.jpg"
recommendations: "Consider adding a note about damp tinder."
duration_minutes: 30
verified: false
```

---

**`scripts/validate_field_tests.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate all field-test reports against the schema."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SCHEMA_PATH = ROOT / "schemas" / "field-test.schema.json"
FIELD_TESTS_DIR = ROOT / "field-tests"


def main() -> int:
    with SCHEMA_PATH.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        schema = json.load(f)
    validator = Draft202012Validator(schema)

    if not FIELD_TESTS_DIR.exists():
        print("No field-tests directory; nothing to validate.")
        return 0

    errors = []
    count = 0
    for path in sorted(FIELD_TESTS_DIR.glob("*.yaml")):
        count += 1
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
        for err in validator.iter_errors(data):
            errors.append(f"{path}: {err.json_path}: {err.message}")

    if errors:
        print(f"Field-test validation failed with {len(errors)} error(s):", file=sys.stderr)
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    print(f"Field-test validation passed. {count} report(s) checked.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## 3.3 Verification Dashboard

**`scripts/dashboard.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Generate a static verification dashboard as a self-contained HTML file.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
INDEX_PATH = ROOT / "build" / "index.db"
SIDECARS_DIR = ROOT / "sidecars"
FIELD_TESTS_DIR = ROOT / "field-tests"
LEDGER_PATH = ROOT / "governance" / "verification-ledger.jsonl"
OUT_PATH = ROOT / "build" / "dashboard.html"

TEMPLATE = """<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>Verification Dashboard — A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World</title>
<style>
:root {
  --fg:#1a1a1a; --bg:#fafaf7; --accent:#8b5a2b; --muted:#666;
  --green:#2e7d32; --yellow:#b28900; --red:#c62828; --blue:#1565c0;
}
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
body {
  font-family: -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif;
  max-width: 72rem; margin: 0 auto; padding: 2rem;
  color: var(--fg); background: var(--bg);
}
h1 { border-bottom: 2px solid var(--accent); padding-bottom: .5rem; }
.grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr)); gap: 1rem; }
.card { background: #fff; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 8px; padding: 1rem; }
.card h3 { margin: 0 0 .5rem; font-size: .85rem; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--muted); }
.card .value { font-size: 2rem; font-weight: 600; }
.bar { height: 12px; border-radius: 6px; background: #eee; overflow: hidden; margin-top: .5rem; }
.bar > span { display: block; height: 100%; }
table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin-top: 1.5rem; }
th, td { border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: .45rem .6rem; text-align: left; font-size: .9rem; }
th { background: #f0ece4; }
.status-draft { color: var(--muted); }
.status-peer-reviewed { color: var(--blue); }
.status-field-tested { color: var(--green); font-weight: 600; }
.status-deprecated { color: var(--red); }
.badge { display: inline-block; padding: .1rem .5rem; border-radius: 3px; font-size: .75rem; }
.badge.green { background: #d4edda; color: #155724; }
.badge.yellow { background: #fff3cd; color: #856404; }
.badge.red { background: #f8d7da; color: #721c24; }
footer { margin-top: 2rem; color: var(--muted); font-size: .8rem; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>Verification Dashboard</h1>
<p>Generated {generated_at}</p>

<div class="grid">
  <div class="card"><h3>Total Entries</h3><div class="value">{total}</div></div>
  <div class="card"><h3>Field-Tested</h3><div class="value" style="color:var(--green)">{field_tested}</div>
    <div class="bar"><span style="width:{pct_field}%; background:var(--green)"></span></div>
    <small>{pct_field}% verified in the field</small>
  </div>
  <div class="card"><h3>Peer-Reviewed</h3><div class="value" style="color:var(--blue)">{peer_reviewed}</div></div>
  <div class="card"><h3>Draft</h3><div class="value" style="color:var(--muted)">{draft}</div></div>
</div>

<h2>Domain Breakdown</h2>
<table>
<thead><tr><th>Domain</th><th>Total</th><th>Draft</th><th>Peer-Reviewed</th><th>Field-Tested</th><th>Deprecated</th><th>% Field-Tested</th></tr></thead>
<tbody>
{domain_rows}
</tbody>
</table>

<h2>Safety Distribution</h2>
<table>
<thead><tr><th>Level</th><th>Count</th></tr></thead>
<tbody>
{safety_rows}
</tbody>
</table>

<h2>Difficulty Distribution</h2>
<table>
<thead><tr><th>Level</th><th>Count</th></tr></thead>
<tbody>
{difficulty_rows}
</tbody>
</table>

<h2>Recent Verification Events</h2>
<table>
<thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Entry</th><th>From</th><th>To</th><th>Actor</th></tr></thead>
<tbody>
{recent_rows}
</tbody>
</table>

<footer>
  <p>A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World — verification dashboard.</p>
</footer>
</body>
</html>
"""


def _load_entries():
    """Yield (sidecar, domain)."""
    for path in sorted(SIDECARS_DIR.rglob("*.pnk.yaml")):
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            yield yaml.safe_load(f)


def _load_ledger():
    if not LEDGER_PATH.exists():
        return []
    events = []
    for line in LEDGER_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            events.append(json.loads(line))
    return events


def build() -> int:
    entries = list(_load_entries())
    total = len(entries)

    by_domain: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    safety_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    difficulty_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    status_counts = {"draft": 0, "peer-reviewed": 0, "field-tested": 0, "deprecated": 0}

    for e in entries:
        domain = e["domain"]
        status = e["verification"]["status"]
        by_domain.setdefault(
            domain,
            {"total": 0, "draft": 0, "peer-reviewed": 0, "field-tested": 0, "deprecated": 0},
        )
        by_domain[domain]["total"] += 1
        by_domain[domain][status] = by_domain[domain].get(status, 0) + 1
        status_counts[status] = status_counts.get(status, 0) + 1
        safety_counts[e["safety"]] = safety_counts.get(e["safety"], 0) + 1
        difficulty_counts[e["difficulty"]] = difficulty_counts.get(e["difficulty"], 0) + 1

    pct_field = round(100 * status_counts["field-tested"] / total, 1) if total else 0

    domain_rows = ""
    for d, c in sorted(by_domain.items()):
        pct = round(100 * c["field-tested"] / c["total"], 1) if c["total"] else 0
        domain_rows += (
            f"<tr><td>{d}</td><td>{c['total']}</td>"
            f"<td>{c.get('draft',0)}</td>"
            f"<td>{c.get('peer-reviewed',0)}</td>"
            f"<td>{c.get('field-tested',0)}</td>"
            f"<td>{c.get('deprecated',0)}</td>"
            f"<td>{pct}%</td></tr>\n"
        )

    safety_rows = "\n".join(
        f"<tr><td><span class='badge {k}'>{k}</span></td><td>{v}</td></tr>"
        for k, v in sorted(safety_counts.items())
    )
    difficulty_rows = "\n".join(
        f"<tr><td>{k}</td><td>{v}</td></tr>"
        for k, v in sorted(difficulty_counts.items())
    )

    events = sorted(_load_ledger(), key=lambda x: x["timestamp"], reverse=True)[:25]
    recent_rows = "\n".join(
        f"<tr><td>{e['timestamp']}</td><td>{e['entry_id']}</td>"
        f"<td class='status-{e['from_status']}'>{e['from_status']}</td>"
        f"<td class='status-{e['to_status']}'>{e['to_status']}</td>"
        f"<td>{e['actor']}</td></tr>"
        for e in events
    )

    html = TEMPLATE.format(
        generated_at=datetime.utcnow().isoformat(timespec="seconds") + "Z",
        total=total,
        field_tested=status_counts["field-tested"],
        peer_reviewed=status_counts["peer-reviewed"],
        draft=status_counts["draft"],
        pct_field=pct_field,
        domain_rows=domain_rows,
        safety_rows=safety_rows,
        difficulty_rows=difficulty_rows,
        recent_rows=recent_rows,
    )

    OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT_PATH.write_text(html, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Dashboard written: {OUT_PATH}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    sys.exit(build())
```

## 3.4 Governance Charters

**`governance/CHARTER.md`**
```markdown
# Governance Charter — A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World

## Article I — Purpose

A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World exists to preserve and distribute the
knowledge needed to rebuild civilization from first principles. This charter
defines how decisions are made, how contributors are empowered, and how the
project remains free and open.

## Article II — Principles

1. **Openness.** All content, code, and governance are public.
2. **Verifiability.** Claims are sourced; procedures are field-tested.
3. **Decentralization.** Anyone may fork the project without permission.
4. **Subsidiarity.** Decisions are made at the lowest competent level.
5. **Non-violence.** Discourse is respectful; harassment is prohibited.
6. **Stewardship.** The project is held in trust for future generations.

## Article III — Membership

Any person may become a **Contributor** by signing the Contributor License
Agreement (CLA) and submitting a pull request.

Contributors who demonstrate sustained quality may be nominated to one of
the following roles by a Domain Maintainer:

- **Verifier** — field-tests entries and certifies them.
- **Domain Maintainer** — reviews pull requests in one domain.
- **Core Maintainer** — release management, architecture, governance.

## Article IV — Roles & Responsibilities

### Readers
- Browse, search, download, mirror.

### Contributors
- Submit entries, edits, translations, tests, bug fixes.
- Follow the Code of Conduct.
- Sign the CLA.

### Verifiers
- Execute entries in the field.
- Submit field-test reports using the schema.
- Recuse themselves from reviewing their own entries.

### Domain Maintainers
- Review and merge pull requests for one domain.
- Nominate new contributors for verifier status.
- Maintain the domain README index.
- Serve a 12-month term, renewable by consensus of peers.

### Core Maintainers
- Merge cross-domain changes.
- Cut releases (semver).
- Manage CI, security, and infrastructure.
- Ratify charter amendments.
- Minimum of 3, maximum of 9.

## Article V — Decision Making

### Default: Rough Consensus

Decisions are made by rough consensus of the relevant maintainers. Silence
is consent after a 7-day comment period.

### Escalation: Formal Vote

If consensus fails, a formal vote is held:

- Simple majority for operational decisions.
- Two-thirds supermajority for charter amendments.
- Unanimity for changing the license.

Each Core Maintainer has one vote. Domain Maintainers vote on issues in
their domain. Contributors may call for a vote via petition signed by 10
contributors.

### Conflict Resolution

1. Good-faith discussion between parties.
2. Mediation by a Domain Maintainer.
3. Mediation by two Core Maintainers.
4. Binding arbitration by a three-member panel selected by lot.

## Article VI — Verification

1. An entry begins in **draft**.
2. Two reviewers advance it to **peer-reviewed**.
3. A field test advances it to **field-tested**.
4. If superseded, it becomes **deprecated**.
5. All transitions are recorded in the verification ledger.

## Article VII — Licensing

- Content: CC BY-SA 4.0.
- Code: MIT.
- Domain names, logos: reserved, but may be used by mirrors with attribution.

Forks may relicense derivative content under any compatible license.

## Article VIII — Finances

The project accepts donations through an Open Collective or fiscal sponsor.
Funds are used exclusively for:

- Infrastructure (CI, hosting, domain).
- Community tooling.
- Travel stipends for verification field tests.

Financial reports are published quarterly.

## Article IX — Amendments

This charter may be amended by a two-thirds vote of Core Maintainers, after
a 14-day public comment period.

## Article X — Dissolution

If the project is dissolved, all content is archived with the Internet
Archive and Zenodo under its open licenses. Any remaining assets are donated
to a like-minded nonprofit.

---

**Adopted:** 2026-01-01
**Version:** 1.0.0
```

---

**`governance/ROLES.md`**
```markdown
# Roles & Responsibilities

## Role Matrix

| Action | Reader | Contributor | Verifier | Domain Maintainer | Core Maintainer |
|--------|:------:|:-----------:|:--------:|:-----------------:|:---------------:|
| Browse / download | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Submit PR | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Review PR | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Merge PR (own domain) | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Merge PR (any domain) | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ |
| File field test | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Certify entry | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| Cut release | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ |
| Amend charter | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ |
| Appoint Domain Maintainer | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ |
| Appoint Core Maintainer | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ (2/3) |

## Onboarding Path

1. **Reader → Contributor**
   - Read `CONTRIBUTING.md`.
   - Sign the CLA (automated via CLA Assistant bot).
   - Submit a first pull request (a typo fix counts).

2. **Contributor → Verifier**
   - Submit 3 merged PRs of substantive content.
   - Complete a field test using the schema.
   - Nominated by a Domain Maintainer.

3. **Verifier → Domain Maintainer**
   - 6 months of active verification.
   - Nominated by two peers.
   - Approved by consensus of Domain Maintainers for that domain.

4. **Domain Maintainer → Core Maintainer**
   - 12 months of active maintainership.
   - Nominated by a Core Maintainer.
   - Approved by 2/3 vote of Core Maintainers.

## Term Limits

- Domain Maintainers serve 12-month renewable terms.
- Core Maintainers serve 24-month renewable terms.
- No single person may hold more than one role at a time.

## Recusal

- Reviewers must recuse from entries they authored.
- Verifiers must recuse from field-testing their own entries.
- Core Maintainers must recuse from decisions that financially benefit them.
```

**`governance/DECISION-LOG.md`**
```markdown
# Decision Log

Every substantive decision is recorded here. Format: `YYYY-MM-DD — Title`.

---

### 2026-01-01 — Charter adopted
**Decision:** Adopt version 1.0.0 of the governance charter.
**Context:** Initial bootstrap; ratified by founding maintainers.
**Vote:** Unanimous (5/5).

### 2026-01-15 — Domain structure ratified
**Decision:** Eight domains: survival, agriculture, materials, energy,
construction, health, knowledge, society.
**Context:** Ensures no domain becomes a dumping ground; each has a
maintainer.
**Vote:** 5/5.

### 2026-02-01 — Verification lifecycle fixed
**Decision:** Four states: draft → peer-reviewed → field-tested → deprecated.
**Context:** Prevents ambiguity in the verification ledger.
**Vote:** 5/5.

### 2026-03-01 — PNK namespace frozen
**Decision:** `pnk:` identifiers are stable and opaque; they are never
rewritten even if domains change.
**Context:** Preserves cross-KB links across forks.
**Vote:** 4/5 (one abstention).
```

## 3.5 Contributor Onboarding Automation

**`.github/workflows/onboarding.yml`**
```yaml
name: Contributor Onboarding

on:
  pull_request_target:
    types: [opened]
  issues:
    types: [opened]

permissions:
  contents: read
  pull-requests: write
  issues: write

jobs:
  welcome:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Welcome first-time contributors
        uses: actions/first-interaction@v1
        with:
          repo-token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          pr-message: |
            Thank you for your first pull request to **A Book Of Life To
            Rebuild The World**!

            A maintainer will review your change within 7 days. In the
            meantime, please confirm:

            - [ ] Your sidecar validates (`python scripts/validate.py`)
            - [ ] You signed the CLA (the bot will prompt you)
            - [ ] You cited every source with a license
            - [ ] You assigned a safety level (green/yellow/red)

            See `CONTRIBUTING.md` for details.
          issue-message: |
            Thank you for opening your first issue in **A Book Of Life To
            Rebuild The World**!

            A maintainer will respond within 7 days. Please include:
            - The domain (survival, agriculture, materials, energy,
              construction, health, knowledge, society)
            - Your sources
            - Your safety assessment

  cla-check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: contributor-assistant/github-action@v2.4.0
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLA_BOT_TOKEN }}
        with:
          path-to-signatures: "governance/cla-signatures.json"
          path-to-document: "https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/blob/main/governance/CLA.md"
          branch: "main"
          allowlist: "dependabot[bot],github-actions[bot]"

  label:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/labeler@v5
        with:
          repo-token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
```

## 3.6 Review Workflow (Automated)

**`.github/workflows/review.yml`**
```yaml
name: Review Workflow

on:
  pull_request:
    types: [opened, synchronize, labeled]

permissions:
  contents: read
  pull-requests: write

jobs:
  require-reviewers:
    if: contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'peer-review')
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - name: Check reviewer count
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        run: |
          PR=${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
          REVIEWS=$(gh pr view $PR --json reviews -q '.reviews | map(select(.state == "APPROVED")) | length')
          echo "Approved reviews: $REVIEWS"
          if [ "$REVIEWS" -lt 2 ]; then
            echo "::error::Peer review requires at least 2 approvals."
            exit 1
          fi
      - name: Validate all sidecars
        run: python scripts/validate.py
      - name: Validate field tests
        run: python scripts/validate_field_tests.py
      - name: Build index
        run: python scripts/build_index.py

  auto-merge-if-verified:
    if: |
      contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'field-tested') &&
      github.actor != 'dependabot[bot]'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: pascalgn/automerge-action@v0.16.4
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
          MERGE_LABELS: "field-tested,!do-not-merge"
          MERGE_METHOD: "squash"
          MERGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE: "pull-request-title"
          MERGE_RETRIES: "6"
          MERGE_RETRY_SLEEP: "10000"
```

## 3.7 Verification API Endpoints (Extended)

**`scripts/serve.py`** (extended)
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""FastAPI server exposing search, retrieval, and verification."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, HTMLResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel

import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
from search import search as hybrid_search  # noqa: E402
from verify import (  # noqa: E402
    Status,
    list_field_tests,
    load_ledger,
    load_record,
)
from dashboard import OUT_PATH as DASHBOARD_PATH  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
INDEX_PATH = ROOT / "build" / "index.db"

app = FastAPI(title="A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World API", version="1.0.0")


class SearchResult(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    domain: str
    difficulty: str
    safety: str
    score: float


class Entry(BaseModel):
    id: str
    title: str
    domain: str
    tags: str
    difficulty: str
    safety: str
    verification_status: str
    body: str


class VerificationResponse(BaseModel):
    entry_id: str
    status: str
    reviewers: list[str]
    tested_by: str | None
    tested_date: str | None
    tested_location: str | None
    notes: str


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"status": "ok", "index_exists": INDEX_PATH.exists()}


@app.get("/search", response_model=list[SearchResult])
def search_endpoint(
    q: str = Query(..., min_length=1),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
):
    return hybrid_search(q, limit)


@app.get("/entry/{entry_id:path}", response_model=Entry)
def get_entry(entry_id: str):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(INDEX_PATH)
    try:
        row = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT id, title, domain, tags, difficulty, safety,
                   verification_status, body
            FROM entries WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (entry_id,),
        ).fetchone()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Entry not found")
    return Entry(
        id=row[0], title=row[1], domain=row[2], tags=row[3],
        difficulty=row[4], safety=row[5], verification_status=row[6], body=row[7],
    )


@app.get("/verify/{entry_id:path}", response_model=VerificationResponse)
def get_verification(entry_id: str):
    try:
        rec = load_record(entry_id)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(e))
    return VerificationResponse(
        entry_id=rec.entry_id,
        status=rec.status.value,
        reviewers=rec.reviewers,
        tested_by=rec.tested_by,
        tested_date=rec.tested_date,
        tested_location=rec.tested_location,
        notes=rec.notes,
    )


@app.get("/field-tests")
def get_field_tests():
    return list_field_tests()


@app.get("/ledger")
def get_ledger():
    return load_ledger()


@app.get("/stats")
def get_stats():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(INDEX_PATH)
    try:
        total = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entries").fetchone()[0]
        by_status = dict(
            conn.execute(
                "SELECT verification_status, COUNT(*) FROM entries GROUP BY verification_status"
            ).fetchall()
        )
        by_domain = dict(
            conn.execute("SELECT domain, COUNT(*) FROM entries GROUP BY domain").fetchall()
        )
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return {
        "total": total,
        "by_status": by_status,
        "by_domain": by_domain,
    }


@app.get("/dashboard", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def dashboard():
    if DASHBOARD_PATH.exists():
        return DASHBOARD_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=404,
        detail="Dashboard not built. Run: python scripts/dashboard.py",
    )


@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def index():
    return """
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html><head><title>A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World</title></head>
    <body>
      <h1>A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World — API</h1>
      <ul>
        <li><a href="/docs">/docs</a> — OpenAPI spec</li>
        <li><a href="/dashboard">/dashboard</a> — verification dashboard</li>
        <li><a href="/stats">/stats</a> — knowledge base stats</li>
      </ul>
    </body></html>
    """
```

---

## 3.8 MCP Verification Tools (Extended)

Add these handlers to `scripts/mcp_server.py` in the tool registry and dispatcher:

```python
# Add to list_tools():
Tool(
    name="abook_ledger",
    description="Return the append-only verification ledger (most recent 100 events).",
    inputSchema={"type": "object", "properties": {}},
),
Tool(
    name="abook_field_tests",
    description="List all field-test reports with their entry ids, testers, dates, and locations.",
    inputSchema={"type": "object", "properties": {}},
),
Tool(
    name="abook_coverage",
    description="Return verification coverage by domain: total, draft, peer-reviewed, field-tested.",
    inputSchema={"type": "object", "properties": {}},
),
```

```python
# Add to call_tool() dispatcher:
if name == "abook_ledger":
    from verify import load_ledger
    events = load_ledger()[-100:]
    return [TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"events": events}, indent=2))]

if name == "abook_field_tests":
    from verify import list_field_tests
    return [TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"field_tests": list_field_tests()}, indent=2))]

if name == "abook_coverage":
    conn = _conn()
    try:
        rows = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT domain, verification_status, COUNT(*)
            FROM entries GROUP BY domain, verification_status
            """
        ).fetchall()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    coverage: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for domain, status, count in rows:
        coverage.setdefault(domain, {})[status] = count
    return [TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"coverage": coverage}, indent=2))]
```

## 3.9 Example Field Test

**`field-tests/ft-making-fire-arizona-2026-03.yaml`**
```yaml
id: ft-making-fire-arizona-2026-03
entry_id: pnk:01HZX0K8J9Q7R3VY6T2M4B8N1P/survival/making-fire
tester: Community Team Alpha
tester_contact: alpha@abookoflife.org
date: "2026-03-15"
location: "Sonoran Desert, Arizona, USA"
gps: "33.4484, -112.0740"
conditions:
  weather: clear, dry, light breeze
  temperature_c: 27
  altitude_m: 340
  materials_substituted: none
  tools_available: [ferro rod, bow drill set, flint and steel]
procedure_followed: "Tested all three methods in sequence."
deviations: "Used mesquite for the fireboard instead of the listed softwood."
results: |
  Ferro rod ignited tinder in 45 seconds on first try.
  Bow drill produced an ember in 3 minutes 20 seconds; required 4 attempts.
  Flint and steel required char cloth; without it, no ignition.
measurements:
  - name: ferro_rod_ignition_time
    value: "45"
    unit: seconds
  - name: bow_drill_ember_time
    value: "200"
    unit: seconds
  - name: flint_steel_with_charcloth
    value: "30"
    unit: seconds
failure_modes: |
  Bow drill spindle slipped twice due to insufficient downward pressure.
  Flint and steel failed entirely without char cloth.
safety_observed: true
safety_notes: "No injuries. Fire clear of overhanging branches."
photos:
  - "field-tests/ft-making-fire-arizona-2026-03/photo-01.jpg"
  - "field-tests/ft-making-fire-arizona-2026-03/photo-02.jpg"
recommendations: |
  Add a note that bow drill requires 2-3 kg of downward pressure.
  Emphasize char cloth production before flint-and-steel attempts.
duration_minutes: 45
verified: true
```

.PHONY: help validate index bundle dashboard verify serve mcp rag test clean

help:
	@echo "A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World"
	@echo ""
	@echo "  make validate    Validate all sidecars + field tests"
	@echo "  make index       Build the hybrid search index"
	@echo "  make bundle      Build the offline HTML + ZIM bundle"
	@echo "  make dashboard   Build the verification dashboard"
	@echo "  make verify      Advance a verification state (see scripts/verify.py)"
	@echo "  make serve       Run the local API server"
	@echo "  make mcp         Run the MCP server (stdio)"
	@echo "  make rag         Query the RAG layer"
	@echo "  make test        Run the full test suite"
	@echo "  make clean       Remove build artifacts"

validate:
	python scripts/validate.py
	python scripts/validate_field_tests.py

index:
	python scripts/build_index.py

bundle:
	python scripts/build_zim.py

dashboard:
	python scripts/dashboard.py

verify:
	@echo "Usage: python scripts/verify.py show <entry_id>"
	@echo "       python scripts/verify.py advance <entry_id> --to <status> --actor <you>"

serve:
	python scripts/serve.py

mcp:
	python scripts/mcp_server.py

rag:
	@echo "Usage: python scripts/rag.py \"your question\""

test:
	pytest -v

clean:
	rm -rf build/ __pycache__/ .pytest_cache/ scripts/__pycache__/ tests/__pycache__/
```

name: CI

      - name: Build dashboard
        run: python scripts/dashboard.py

        run: pytest -v

            build/index.db

          args: --no-progress --exclude-mail 'domains/**/*.md'
          fail: true

## 3.12 Verification Dashboard Preview

Running `make dashboard` produces `build/dashboard.html` with:

- **Top cards:** total entries, field-tested count + %, peer-reviewed count, draft count
- **Domain breakdown table:** per-domain totals with status breakdown and % field-tested
- **Safety distribution:** green / yellow / red counts
- **Difficulty distribution:** novice → master counts
- **Recent verification events:** last 25 transitions from the ledger

Accessible live at `http://localhost:8080/dashboard` when the API is running.

## Phase 3 Delivery Summary

| Artifact | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `scripts/verify.py` | Verification state machine + CLI (`abook-verify`) |
| `scripts/validate_field_tests.py` | Field-test schema validator |
| `scripts/dashboard.py` | Static verification dashboard generator |
| `schemas/field-test.schema.json` | Field-test JSON schema |
| `schemas/field-test-template.yaml` | Field-test starter template |
| `field-tests/ft-making-fire-arizona-2026-03.yaml` | Complete example field test |
| `governance/CHARTER.md` | Full governance charter (10 articles) |
| `governance/ROLES.md` | Role matrix + onboarding path + term limits |
| `governance/DECISION-LOG.md` | Append-only decision log |
| `governance/CLA.md` | Contributor License Agreement |
| `.github/workflows/onboarding.yml` | First-PR welcome + CLA bot + auto-labeling |
| `.github/workflows/review.yml` | Enforces 2-reviewer rule; auto-merge for field-tested |
| `.github/labeler.yml` | Path-based PR labels |
| `scripts/serve.py` (extended) | Adds `/verify`, `/field-tests`, `/ledger`, `/stats`, `/dashboard` |
| `scripts/mcp_server.py` (extended) | Adds `abook_ledger`, `abook_field_tests`, `abook_coverage` tools |
| `Makefile` | One-command entry points for every workflow |
| `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (updated) | Adds field-test validation, dashboard build, full test run |

**Verification lifecycle enforced end-to-end:**

1. Draft entry created via `abook-new`.
2. Two approvals move it to `peer-reviewed` (enforced by `review.yml`).
3. A `field-tests/ft-*.yaml` report advances it to `field-tested`.
4. Every transition is appended to `governance/verification-ledger.jsonl`.
5. Dashboard reflects coverage instantly.
6. MCP agents can query status via `abook_verify`, `abook_coverage`, and `abook_ledger`.

Say **Next** to receive **Phase 4: Expansion & Enrichment** — including the missing entries referenced by stubs (`chemistry`, `music`, `games`), 3D model integration (OpenSCAD), video/audio pipelines, interactive simulators, i18n framework with 8 languages, and regional variants.

# Phase 4: Expansion & Enrichment

This phase fills the stub references, adds the full media pipeline (3D models, video, audio, simulators), implements the i18n framework with 8 languages, and adds regional variants.

---

## 4.1 Fill Stub Entries

**`domains/materials/chemistry.md`**
```markdown
# Chemistry

> **Domain:** materials | **Difficulty:** journeyman | **Safety:** red

## Overview

Chemistry underpins soap, medicine, metallurgy, and agriculture. This entry
covers the foundational reactions and safety rules needed to produce acids,
bases, salts, and simple organic compounds.

## Prerequisites

- [Metal Smelting](metal-smelting.md)
- [Charcoal Production](charcoal-production.md)

## Materials & Tools

- Glass vessels (see [Glass Making](glass-making.md))
- Heat source (charcoal forge)
- Acids: vinegar (acetic), citric (lemon), sulfuric (from sulfide ores)
- Bases: lime (CaO), wood ash (K₂CO₃), ammonia (from urine)
- Litmus or red cabbage indicator

## Procedure

### Safety First

1. Always add acid to water, never water to acid.
2. Wear eye protection and gloves.
3. Work outdoors or under a fume hood.
4. Have a bucket of water and baking soda within reach.

### Making Sulfuric Acid (Lead Chamber)

1. Burn sulfur in a lead-lined chamber with moist air.
2. SO₂ + O₂ + H₂O → H₂SO₄.
3. Collect the acid at the chamber base.
4. Concentrate by boiling off water.

### Making Nitric Acid (Saltpeter + Sulfuric)

1. Mix saltpeter (KNO₃) with sulfuric acid in a glass retort.
2. Heat gently; distill nitric acid into a cooled receiver.
3. Store in dark glass.

### Making Hydrochloric Acid (Salt + Sulfuric)

1. Mix table salt with sulfuric acid.
2. Collect HCl gas in water.
3. Store in glass.

### Making Lye (Potassium Hydroxide)

1. Leach hardwood ash with rainwater.
2. Evaporate to concentrate.
3. Test with a fresh egg — it floats when strong enough.

### Making Soap (Saponification)

See [Soap Making](../survival/soap-making.md).

### Making Gunpowder

1. Mix 75% potassium nitrate, 15% charcoal, 10% sulfur by weight.
2. Mill to a fine powder.
3. **Red hazard:** Static sparks ignite powder. Work with wooden tools, ground yourself.

### pH Testing (Red Cabbage Indicator)

1. Boil red cabbage; save the purple liquid.
2. Add a drop of test solution.
3. Red = acid, green = base, purple = neutral.

### Common Reactions

- **Neutralization:** acid + base → salt + water.
- **Oxidation:** metal + oxygen → oxide.
- **Reduction:** oxide + carbon → metal + CO₂.
- **Combustion:** fuel + O₂ → CO₂ + H₂O.

## Safety Notes

- **Red hazard:** Concentrated acids and bases destroy tissue on contact.
- **Red hazard:** Many reactions produce toxic or explosive gases.
- Never taste or smell chemicals directly.
- Never store acids in metal containers.
- Ventilate always.

## Verification

- **Status:** draft
- **Date:** 2026-07-18

## Sources

- US Army Chemistry Manual (public domain)
- Historical chemistry texts (public domain)
```

**`domains/knowledge/music.md`**
```markdown
# Music

> **Domain:** knowledge | **Difficulty:** apprentice | **Safety:** green

## Overview

Music is a universal human language. It binds communities, transmits stories,
and heals trauma. Instruments can be built from local materials.

## Prerequisites

- [Simple Tools](../survival/simple-tools.md)

## Materials & Tools

- Wood, gut, hide, bamboo, gourd
- Metal (for strings, bells)
- Voice

## Procedure

### Rhythm First

1. Clapping patterns: 4/4, 3/4, 6/8.
2. Call and response.
3. Polyrhythms: two patterns at once.

### Drums

- **Frame drum:** hide stretched over a wooden hoop.
- **Djembe:** goblet-shaped, rope-tuned.
- **Log drum:** hollowed log with slits.

### Jaw Harp

1. Shape a metal or bamboo tongue in a frame.
2. Pluck the tongue while changing mouth shape.
3. Mouth cavity amplifies harmonics.

### Flutes

- **Bamboo flute:** cut a tube, bore finger holes, notch the mouthpiece.
- **Pan pipes:** graduated tubes bound together.
- **Ocarina:** closed vessel with finger holes.

### String Instruments

- **Berimbau:** bow with a gourd resonator.
- **Lyre:** U-shaped frame with strings.
- **Guitar:** box with neck and frets.

### Music Theory (Basic)

1. **Scale:** 7 notes per octave (do re mi fa sol la ti).
2. **Intervals:** distance between notes.
3. **Chords:** three or more notes together.
4. **Keys:** major (bright), minor (dark).

### Singing

1. Breathe from the diaphragm.
2. Open the throat.
3. Match pitch with an instrument.
4. Sing in rounds, harmonies, and unison.

### Songs for Community

- Work songs (rhythm for shared labor).
- Lullabies.
- Lamentations.
- Celebration songs.
- Anthems.

### Recording (If Available)

- Wax cylinder, magnetic tape, or digital.
- Preserve for future generations.

## Safety Notes

- Green — but loud sounds damage hearing. Protect ears around drums.
- Singing is safe; hydrate.

## Verification

- **Status:** peer-reviewed
- **Date:** 2026-07-20

## Sources

- Ethnomusicology archives (public domain)
- Traditional instrument-making (public domain)
```

**`domains/society/games.md`**
```markdown
# Games

> **Domain:** society | **Difficulty:** apprentice | **Safety:** green

## Overview

Games transmit strategy, math, and social skills. They are played for joy,
teaching, and community bonding.

## Prerequisites

- [Festivals & Celebration](festivals-celebration.md)

## Materials & Tools

- Dice, stones, sticks, boards, cards
- Two or more players

## Procedure

### Dice

- **Knucklebones:** animal ankle bones.
- **Cubical dice:** carved from wood, bone, or clay.
- **Games:** craps, yatzy, liars' dice.

### Mancala

1. Board with 2 rows of 6 pits + 2 stores.
2. Each pit starts with 4 stones.
3. Sow stones counter-clockwise.
4. Capture opponent's stones.
5. Win by accumulating the most.

### Tigers & Goats

1. 4 tigers vs 20 goats on a 5×5 grid.
2. Goats block; tigers jump to capture.
3. Tigers win by eating 5 goats.
4. Goats win by blocking all tigers.

### Chess

1. 8×8 board.
2. Each side: 8 pawns, 2 rooks, 2 knights, 2 bishops, 1 queen, 1 king.
3. Objective: checkmate the king.
4. Teaches strategy, patience, foresight.

### Go

1. 19×19 board (or 13×13, 9×9 for beginners).
2. Players place black/white stones alternately.
3. Surround territory to capture.
4. Objective: control more territory than opponent.
5. Oldest continuously-played board game.

### Playing Cards

1. 52-card deck (4 suits × 13 ranks).
2. Games: poker, bridge, whist, rummy.
3. Card games teach probability.

### The Game of Life (Simulation)

1. Board with life path.
2. Spin to advance.
3. Choices: career, family, wealth.
4. Teaches consequences.

### Physical Games

- Tag, hide-and-seek, races.
- Wrestling, tug-of-war.
- Ball games: soccer, catch, juggling.

### Teaching Games

- Counting games for children.
- Memory games.
- Strategy games for teens.

### Gambling

- Historically common but harmful.
- If played, keep stakes symbolic.
- Never gamble essentials.

## Safety Notes

- Green — but gambling addiction destroys families. Avoid real stakes.
- Physical games risk injury; warm up and play fairly.
- Chess and Go can become obsessive; balance.

## Verification

- **Status:** peer-reviewed
- **Date:** 2026-07-22

## Sources

- H.J.R. Murray's Board Games (public domain)
- Traditional game rules (public domain)
```

## 4.2 3D Model Pipeline (OpenSCAD)

# 3D Models

Parametric 3D models for tools, machines, and structures described in the
knowledge base. Every model is:

- Written in OpenSCAD (plain text, versionable).
- Parametric (dimensions adjust to local materials).
- Exportable to STL/STEP for printing or CNC.
- Linked to a sidecar via `media:` entries.

## Build all models

```bash
python scripts/build_models.py
```

Output goes to `build/models/`.

## Model index

| Model | Entry | Parameters |
|-------|-------|------------|
| `bow_drill.scad` | survival/making-fire | spindle_len, board_width |
| `water_filter.scad` | survival/water-purification | bottle_dia, layer_heights |
| `rocket_stove.scad` | energy/fire-combustion | feed_w, riser_h |
| `hand_pump.scad` | construction/water-pumps | cylinder_dia, stroke |
| `pelton_wheel.scad` | energy/micro-hydro | wheel_dia, buckets |
| `wind_turbine_blade.scad` | energy/wind-power | length, twist, chord |
| `solar_cooker.scad` | energy/solar-thermal | box_w, box_h, flap_angle |
| `bee_hive.scad` | agriculture/beekeeping | frame_count, box_w |
| `printing_press.scad` | knowledge/printing-press | platen_w, platen_h |
| `loom.scad` | materials/textiles | warp_width, heddle_count |

**`models/survival/bow_drill.scad`**
```openscad
// Bow Drill — Parametric Model
// Entry: pnk:01HZX0K8J9Q7R3VY6T2M4B8N1P/survival/making-fire
// License: MIT
//
// Adjust dimensions to local materials.

spindle_len = 200;   // mm
spindle_dia = 20;    // mm
board_width = 120;   // mm
board_thick = 20;    // mm
bow_len     = 500;   // mm
bow_curve   = 40;    // mm

module spindle() {
    cylinder(h=spindle_len, d=spindle_dia, $fn=64);
    translate([0, 0, spindle_len])
        sphere(d=spindle_dia * 0.8, $fn=32);
    translate([0, 0, -spindle_dia * 0.4])
        sphere(d=spindle_dia * 0.8, $fn=32);
}

module fireboard() {
    difference() {
        cube([board_width, board_width * 0.6, board_thick], center=true);
        // Depression for spindle
        translate([0, 0, board_thick / 2])
            cylinder(h=board_thick * 0.3, d=spindle_dia * 1.1, $fn=48);
        // Notch for ember
        translate([board_width * 0.3, 0, 0])
            rotate([0, 90, 0])
                cylinder(h=board_width, d=spindle_dia * 0.5, $fn=32);
    }
}

module bow() {
    // Bow as a curved rod
    translate([-bow_len / 2, 0, 0])
        for (i = [0 : bow_len / 10 : bow_len])
            hull() {
                translate([i, sin(i / bow_len * 180) * bow_curve, 0])
                    sphere(d=12, $fn=16);
                translate([i + bow_len / 10, sin((i + bow_len / 10) / bow_len * 180) * bow_curve, 0])
                    sphere(d=12, $fn=16);
            }
}

module socket() {
    difference() {
        cylinder(h=40, d=spindle_dia * 1.5, $fn=48);
        translate([0, 0, 10])
            cylinder(h=30, d=spindle_dia * 0.9, $fn=48);
    }
}

// Layout
translate([-spindle_len * 0.6, 0, 0]) spindle();
translate([spindle_len * 0.3, 0, 0]) fireboard();
translate([0, board_width, 0]) bow();
translate([spindle_len * 0.3, -board_width, 0]) socket();
```

---

**`models/energy/rocket_stove.scad`**
```openscad
// Rocket Stove — Parametric Model
// Entry: pnk:01HZX0K8J9Q7R3VY6T2M4B8N40/energy/fire-combustion
// License: MIT

feed_w       = 100;   // mm
feed_h       = 100;   // mm
feed_len     = 300;   // mm
riser_h      = 400;   // mm
riser_w      = 100;   // mm
wall         = 8;     // mm
insulation   = 30;    // mm

module shell() {
    difference() {
        // Outer barrel
        cylinder(h=riser_h + feed_h, d=riser_w + 2*(wall + insulation), $fn=64);
        // Inner riser
        translate([0, 0, feed_h])
            cylinder(h=riser_h, d=riser_w, $fn=64);
        // Feed tube opening
        translate([-feed_w / 2 - wall, -feed_w / 2, -1])
            cube([feed_w + wall * 2, feed_w + wall * 2, feed_h + 1]);
        translate([-feed_w / 2, -feed_w / 2 - wall, 0])
            cube([feed_w, feed_w + wall, feed_h]);
    }
}

module feed_tube() {
    difference() {
        cube([feed_w + 2*wall, feed_w + 2*wall, feed_h + 2*wall]);
        translate([wall, wall, wall])
            cube([feed_w, feed_w, feed_h + wall]);
    }
}

translate([0, 0, 0]) feed_tube();
translate([feed_w / 2 + wall, feed_w / 2 + wall, feed_h / 2]) shell();
```

---

**`models/energy/wind_turbine_blade.scad`**
```openscad
// Wind Turbine Blade — Parametric
// Entry: pnk:01HZX0K8J9Q7R3VY6T2M4B8N43/energy/wind-power
// License: MIT

blade_len    = 1000;  // mm
root_chord   = 120;   // mm
tip_chord    = 40;    // mm
thickness    = 12;    // mm
twist_deg    = 20;    // deg from root to tip

module blade() {
    steps = 20;
    for (i = [0 : steps - 1]) {
        t0 = i / steps;
        t1 = (i + 1) / steps;
        hull() {
            translate([0, 0, t0 * blade_len])
                rotate([0, 0, -t0 * twist_deg])
                    scale([1, 1, 1])
                        cube([root_chord * (1 - t0) + tip_chord * t0,
                              thickness,
                              1], center=true);
            translate([0, 0, t1 * blade_len])
                rotate([0, 0, -t1 * twist_deg])
                    cube([root_chord * (1 - t1) + tip_chord * t1,
                          thickness,
                          1], center=true);
        }
    }
}

blade();
```

---

**`scripts/build_models.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Build all OpenSCAD models into STL/STEP artifacts.
Requires `openscad` on PATH for STL export.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
MODELS_DIR = ROOT / "models"
BUILD_DIR = ROOT / "build" / "models"


def find_openscad() -> str | None:
    for name in ("openscad", "openscad-nightly"):
        if shutil.which(name):
            return name
    return None


def build_model(scad: Path, openscad: str) -> Path:
    rel = scad.relative_to(MODELS_DIR)
    out = BUILD_DIR / rel.with_suffix(".stl")
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    cmd = [
        openscad,
        "-o", str(out),
        "--export-format", "binstl",
        str(scad),
    ]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
    return out


def main() -> int:
    models = sorted(MODELS_DIR.rglob("*.scad"))
    if not models:
        print("No .scad models found.")
        return 0

    openscad = find_openscad()
    if not openscad:
        print("openscad not found on PATH; skipping STL export.")
        print(f"Found {len(models)} .scad source file(s).")
        # Still emit a manifest of source files
        manifest = {
            "source_count": len(models),
            "sources": [str(m.relative_to(ROOT)) for m in models],
            "stl_generated": False,
        }
        (ROOT / "build").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (ROOT / "build" / "models-manifest.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        return 0

    if BUILD_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(BUILD_DIR)
    BUILD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True)

    built = []
    for m in models:
        out = build_model(m, openscad)
        built.append(str(out.relative_to(ROOT)))
        print(f"  {m.relative_to(MODELS_DIR)} -> {out.relative_to(ROOT)}")

    manifest = {
        "source_count": len(models),
        "built_count": len(built),
        "stl_generated": True,
        "artifacts": built,
    }
    (ROOT / "build" / "models-manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(f"Built {len(built)} model(s) into {BUILD_DIR}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## 4.3 Video & Audio Pipeline

**`media/README.md`**
```markdown
# Media Library

Offline-storable video and audio for procedures that are easier to show
than to describe.

## Structure

```
media/
├── video/
│   └── <domain>/
│       └── <entry-slug>/
│           ├── tutorial.mp4
│           ├── tutorial.vtt       # WebVTT transcript
│           └── metadata.yaml
└── audio/
    └── <domain>/
        └── <entry-slug>/
            ├── guide.mp3
            ├── guide.txt          # plain text transcript
            └── metadata.yaml
```

## Metadata schema

See `schemas/media.schema.json`.

## Encoding standards

- Video: H.264, 720p, 30 fps, AAC audio, ≤100 MB per file.
- Audio: MP3, 96 kbps mono, ≤20 MB per file.
- Transcripts: WebVTT for video, plain text for audio.

## Generation

```bash
python scripts/build_media.py
```

This transcribes, indexes, and links media into the offline bundle.
```

---

**`schemas/media.schema.json`**
```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://abookoflife.org/schemas/media.schema.json",
  "title": "Media Metadata",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["entry_id", "type", "path", "license"],
  "properties": {
    "entry_id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^pnk:" },
    "type": { "type": "string", "enum": ["video", "audio", "3d-model", "simulator", "diagram", "photo"] },
    "path": { "type": "string" },
    "transcript": { "type": "string" },
    "duration_seconds": { "type": "number" },
    "language": { "type": "string", "default": "en" },
    "license": { "type": "string" },
    "author": { "type": "string" },
    "date": { "type": "string", "format": "date" },
    "caption": { "type": "string" }
  }
}
```

entry_id: pnk:01HZX0K8J9Q7R3VY6T2M4B8N1P/survival/making-fire

transcript: media/video/survival/making-fire/tutorial.vtt
