```

---

**`scripts/build_media.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Scan media/ for videos and audio, validate metadata, and emit an index
for inclusion in the offline bundle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
MEDIA_DIR = ROOT / "media"
SCHEMA_PATH = ROOT / "schemas" / "media.schema.json"
BUILD_DIR = ROOT / "build"


def _validate(meta: dict, validator: Draft202012Validator, path: Path) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    for err in validator.iter_errors(meta):
        errors.append(f"{path}: {err.json_path}: {err.message}")
    return errors


def main() -> int:
    if not MEDIA_DIR.exists():
        print("No media directory found.")
        return 0

    with SCHEMA_PATH.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        schema = json.load(f)
    validator = Draft202012Validator(schema)

    index = []
    errors: list[str] = []
    count = 0

    for meta_path in sorted(MEDIA_DIR.rglob("metadata.yaml")):
        count += 1
        with meta_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            meta = yaml.safe_load(f)
        errors.extend(_validate(meta, validator, meta_path))

        media_path = ROOT / meta["path"]
        if not media_path.exists():
            errors.append(f"{meta_path}: media file not found: {meta['path']}")
            continue

        if meta.get("transcript"):
            tpath = ROOT / meta["transcript"]
            if not tpath.exists():
                errors.append(f"{meta_path}: transcript not found: {meta['transcript']}")

        index.append(
            {
                "entry_id": meta["entry_id"],
                "type": meta["type"],
                "path": meta["path"],
                "transcript": meta.get("transcript"),
                "language": meta.get("language", "en"),
                "duration_seconds": meta.get("duration_seconds"),
                "caption": meta.get("caption", ""),
            }
        )

    if errors:
        print(f"Media validation failed with {len(errors)} error(s):", file=sys.stderr)
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    BUILD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (BUILD_DIR / "media-index.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"count": count, "items": index}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(f"Media index built: {count} item(s).")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

# Interactive Simulators

Offline-capable, single-file HTML simulators for systems that are easier to

<body>
<h1>Crop Rotation Simulator</h1>
<p>Rotate the four families across four beds so no family returns to the
same bed within 4 years.</p>

<div class="family-key">
  <span class="legume">Legume (fixes N)</span>
  <span class="brassica">Brassica (heavy feeder)</span>
  <span class="root">Root (loosens)</span>
  <span class="fruiting">Fruiting (heavy feeder)</span>
</div>

<table id="grid"></table>
<button onclick="rotate()">Rotate Year</button>
<button onclick="reset()">Reset</button>
<p id="status"></p>

<script>
const FAMILIES = ["legume", "brassica", "root", "fruiting"];
const NAMES = { legume: "Legume", brassica: "Brassica", root: "Root", fruiting: "Fruiting" };
let year = 0;

function reset() { year = 0; render(); }
function rotate() { year = (year + 1) % 4; render(); }

function render() {
  const grid = document.getElementById("grid");
  grid.innerHTML = "";
  const header = document.createElement("tr");
  header.innerHTML = "<th>Year</th><th>Bed 1</th><th>Bed 2</th><th>Bed 3</th><th>Bed 4</th>";
  grid.appendChild(header);
  for (let y = 0; y < 4; y++) {
    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
    tr.innerHTML = `<th>${y + 1}</th>`;
    for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) {
      const fam = FAMILIES[(b + y) % 4];
      const td = document.createElement("td");
      td.className = fam;
      td.textContent = NAMES[fam];
      tr.appendChild(td);
    }
    grid.appendChild(tr);
  }
  document.getElementById("status").textContent = `Current year: ${year + 1}`;

  // Emit snapshot for embedding
  window.abookSnapshot = {
    simulator: "crop-rotation",
    year: year + 1,
    grid: [0,1,2,3].map(y => [0,1,2,3].map(b => FAMILIES[(b + y) % 4])),
  };
}

reset();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

**`simulators/voting.html`**
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>Voting Systems Simulator — A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World</title>
<style>
body { font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; max-width: 52rem; margin: 0 auto;
       padding: 1.5rem; background: #fafaf7; color: #1a1a1a; }
h1 { border-bottom: 2px solid #8b5a2b; padding-bottom: .5rem; }
textarea { width: 100%; height: 8rem; font-family: monospace; padding: .5rem; }
pre { background: #efe9e0; padding: 1rem; border-radius: 6px; overflow-x: auto; }
button { padding: .5rem 1rem; font-size: 1rem; cursor: pointer; margin-right: .5rem; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>Voting Systems Simulator</h1>
<p>Enter ranked ballots (one per line, comma-separated candidates).</p>
<textarea id="ballots">A,B,C
A,C,B
B,C,A
B,A,C
C,A,B
C,B,A</textarea>
<p>
  <button onclick="run()">Run</button>
  <button onclick="loadExample(1)">Example: Spoiler</button>
  <button onclick="loadExample(2)">Example: Consensus</button>
</p>
<pre id="out"></pre>

<script>
function parse() {
  return document.getElementById("ballots").value.trim().split("\n")
    .map(line => line.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean));
}

function fptp(ballots) {
  const tally = {};
  for (const b of ballots) { if (b[0]) tally[b[0]] = (tally[b[0]] || 0) + 1; }
  return { method: "First Past The Post", tally, winner: top(tally) };
}

function approval(ballots) {
  const tally = {};
  for (const b of ballots) for (const c of b) tally[c] = (tally[c] || 0) + 1;
  return { method: "Approval", tally, winner: top(tally) };
}

function borda(ballots) {
  const tally = {};
  for (const b of ballots) {
    b.forEach((c, i) => { tally[c] = (tally[c] || 0) + (b.length - i); });
  }
  return { method: "Borda Count", tally, winner: top(tally) };
}

function instantRunoff(ballots) {
  let candidates = [...new Set(ballots.flat())];
  let round = 0, log = [];
  while (candidates.length > 1) {
    round++;
    const tally = {};
    for (const b of ballots) {
      const first = b.find(c => candidates.includes(c));
      if (first) tally[first] = (tally[first] || 0) + 1;
    }
    log.push({ round, tally: { ...tally } });
    const total = Object.values(tally).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const majority = Object.entries(tally).find(([_, v]) => v > total / 2);
    if (majority) return { method: "Instant Runoff", log, winner: majority[0] };
    const min = Math.min(...candidates.map(c => tally[c] || 0));
    const losers = candidates.filter(c => (tally[c] || 0) === min);
    candidates = candidates.filter(c => !losers.includes(c));
  }
  return { method: "Instant Runoff", log, winner: candidates[0] };
}

function top(tally) {
  return Object.entries(tally).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
}

function run() {
  const ballots = parse();
  const results = [fptp(ballots), approval(ballots), borda(ballots), instantRunoff(ballots)];
  const out = results.map(r =>
    `${r.method}\n  Winner: ${r.winner}\n  Detail: ${JSON.stringify(r.tally || r.log)}`
  ).join("\n\n");
  document.getElementById("out").textContent = out;
  window.abookSnapshot = { simulator: "voting", ballots, results };
}

function loadExample(n) {
  if (n === 1) {
    document.getElementById("ballots").value = "A,B,C\nA,C,B\nB,A,C\nC,B,A\nC,A,B\nB,C,A";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("ballots").value = "A,B,C\nA,B,C\nB,C,A\nC,A,B\nA,C,B";
  }
  run();
}

run();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

## 4.5 i18n Framework

**`i18n/README.md`**
```markdown
# Internationalization (i18n)

A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World supports 8 languages at launch, with a
translation framework that scales to more.

## Supported Languages

| Code | Language | Coverage target |
|------|----------|-----------------|
| `en` | English | 100% |
| `es` | Spanish | 100% |
| `fr` | French | 100% |
| `de` | German | 100% |
| `pt` | Portuguese | 100% |
| `hi` | Hindi | 80% |
| `ar` | Arabic | 80% |
| `sw` | Swahili | 60% |
| `zh` | Chinese (Simplified) | 80% |

## Structure

```
i18n/
├── strings/
│   ├── en.yaml       # canonical UI strings
│   ├── es.yaml
│   ├── fr.yaml
│   └── ...
└── translations/
    ├── es/
    │   └── survival/
    │       └── making-fire.md
    └── ...
```

## Translation workflow

1. A translator copies `domains/<domain>/<slug>.md` to
   `i18n/translations/<lang>/<domain>/<slug>.md`.
2. They translate the body.
3. They create a sidecar at
   `i18n/translations/<lang>/<domain>/<slug>.pnk.yaml` with:
   ```yaml
   translation_of: pnk:...      # original id
   language: es
   ```
4. They run `python scripts/validate_i18n.py` to check coverage.

## Building

`python scripts/build_i18n.py` produces per-language index files and
integrates translations into the offline bundle.
```

---

**`i18n/translations/es/survival/making-fire.md`**
```markdown
# Hacer Fuego

> **Dominio:** supervivencia | **Dificultad:** principiante | **Seguridad:** amarillo

## Descripción general

El fuego proporciona calor, luz, cocina, purificación de agua y protección.
Dominar múltiples métodos de ignición garantiza resiliencia en cualquier entorno.

## Requisitos previos

- Ninguno

## Materiales y herramientas

- Yesca (hierba seca, corteza de abedul, tela carbonizada)
- Astillas (ramitas secas pequeñas)
- Combustible (leña seca más grande)
- Fuente de ignición (uno de los métodos a continuación)

## Procedimiento

### Método 1: Barra de ferrocerio

1. Raspe magnesio de la barra sobre una pequeña pila de yesca.
2. Sostenga el raspador en un ángulo de 45° con la barra.
3. Deslice el raspador por la barra con firmeza para crear chispas.
4. Dirija las chispas hacia la pila de magnesio.
5. Una vez que la yesca se encienda, agregue astillas gradualmente.

### Método 2: Taladro de arco

1. Tallar una tabla de fuego con una depresión poco profunda y una muesca.
2. Cortar un huso de unos 20 cm de largo, puntiagudo en ambos extremos.
3. Hacer un arco con una rama flexible y cordaje.
4. Usar un zócalo (piedra o madera dura) para presionar el huso.
5. Serrar el arco hacia adelante y hacia atrás con presión constante.
6. Cuando la muesca brille, transfiera la brasa a la yesca.

## Notas de seguridad

- Nunca encienda un fuego debajo de ramas colgantes.
- Mantenga una fuente de agua o arena cerca.
- Evite la ropa inflamable al cuidar el fuego.
- No deje fuegos desatendidos.

## Verificación

- **Estado:** borrador
- **Probado por:** —
- **Fecha:** —

## Fuentes

- Manual de campo del Ejército de EE. UU. FM 21-76 (dominio público)
```

**`scripts/validate_i18n.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Validate translation coverage and translation sidecars.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
I18N_DIR = ROOT / "i18n"
STRINGS_DIR = I18N_DIR / "strings"
TRANSLATIONS_DIR = I18N_DIR / "translations"
SCHEMA_PATH = ROOT / "schemas" / "sidecar.schema.json"

LANGS = ["es", "fr", "de", "pt", "hi", "ar", "sw", "zh"]
COVERAGE_TARGETS = {
    "es": 1.0, "fr": 1.0, "de": 1.0, "pt": 1.0,
    "hi": 0.8, "ar": 0.8, "sw": 0.6, "zh": 0.8,
}


def _load_canonical_ids() -> set[str]:
    ids: set[str] = set()
    for path in (ROOT / "sidecars").rglob("*.pnk.yaml"):
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
        ids.add(data["id"])
    return ids


def main() -> int:
    canonical = _load_canonical_ids()
    if not canonical:
        print("No canonical entries found.")
        return 1

    with SCHEMA_PATH.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        schema = json.load(f)
    validator = Draft202012Validator(schema)

    errors: list[str] = []
    coverage: dict[str, float] = {}

    for lang in LANGS:
        # UI strings
        strings_path = STRINGS_DIR / f"{lang}.yaml"
        if not strings_path.exists():
            errors.append(f"Missing UI strings: {strings_path}")
            continue

        with strings_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            strings = yaml.safe_load(f)
        canonical_strings_path = STRINGS_DIR / "en.yaml"
        with canonical_strings_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            canonical_strings = yaml.safe_load(f)
        missing_keys = set(canonical_strings) - set(strings)
        if missing_keys:
            errors.append(f"{lang}: UI strings missing keys: {sorted(missing_keys)}")

        # Content translations
        lang_dir = TRANSLATIONS_DIR / lang
        if not lang_dir.exists():
            coverage[lang] = 0.0
            if COVERAGE_TARGETS[lang] > 0:
                errors.append(f"{lang}: no translations found (target {COVERAGE_TARGETS[lang]:.0%})")
            continue

        translated_ids: set[str] = set()
        for sidecar_path in lang_dir.rglob("*.pnk.yaml"):
            with sidecar_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = yaml.safe_load(f)
            for err in validator.iter_errors(data):
                errors.append(f"{sidecar_path}: {err.json_path}: {err.message}")
            tid = data.get("translation_of") or data.get("id")
            if tid in canonical:
                translated_ids.add(tid)
            else:
                errors.append(f"{sidecar_path}: translation_of {tid} not found in canonical set")

        coverage[lang] = len(translated_ids) / len(canonical)
        if coverage[lang] < COVERAGE_TARGETS[lang]:
            errors.append(
                f"{lang}: coverage {coverage[lang]:.1%} below target {COVERAGE_TARGETS[lang]:.0%}"
            )

    print("Translation coverage:")
    for lang, pct in sorted(coverage.items()):
        print(f"  {lang}: {pct:5.1%} (target {COVERAGE_TARGETS[lang]:.0%})")

    if errors:
        print(f"\ni18n validation failed with {len(errors)} error(s):", file=sys.stderr)
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    print("\ni18n validation passed.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

---

**`scripts/build_i18n.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Build per-language indexes for the offline bundle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
I18N_DIR = ROOT / "i18n"
TRANSLATIONS_DIR = I18N_DIR / "translations"
STRINGS_DIR = I18N_DIR / "strings"
BUILD_DIR = ROOT / "build" / "i18n"

LANGS = ["es", "fr", "de", "pt", "hi", "ar", "sw", "zh"]


def build() -> int:
    BUILD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    manifest: dict[str, dict] = {}

    for lang in LANGS:
        strings_path = STRINGS_DIR / f"{lang}.yaml"
        if not strings_path.exists():
            continue
        with strings_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            strings = yaml.safe_load(f)

        lang_dir = TRANSLATIONS_DIR / lang
        translated: list[dict] = []
        if lang_dir.exists():
            for sidecar_path in sorted(lang_dir.rglob("*.pnk.yaml")):
                with sidecar_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    meta = yaml.safe_load(f)
                md_path = sidecar_path.with_suffix("").with_suffix(".md")
                translated.append(
                    {
                        "id": meta.get("id"),
                        "translation_of": meta.get("translation_of"),
                        "title": meta["title"],
                        "domain": meta["domain"],
                        "path": str(md_path.relative_to(ROOT)),
                    }
                )

        out = {
            "language": lang,
            "strings": strings,
            "translated_count": len(translated),
            "entries": translated,
        }
        out_path = BUILD_DIR / f"{lang}.json"
        out_path.write_text(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        manifest[lang] = {
            "translated_count": len(translated),
            "strings_complete": True,
        }
        print(f"  {lang}: {len(translated)} translation(s)")

    (BUILD_DIR / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(f"i18n build complete: {BUILD_DIR}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(build())
```

---

## 4.6 Regional Variants

**`variants/README.md`**
```markdown
# Regional Variants

Some entries must be adapted to climate, culture, and available materials.
Variants live alongside the canonical entry and override specific sections.

## Structure

```
variants/
├── arid/
│   └── survival/
│       └── water-purification.md
├── tropical/
│   └── agriculture/
│       └── food-preservation.md
├── arctic/
│   └── survival/
│       └── emergency-shelter.md
└── temperate/
    └── construction/
        └── simple-housing.md
```

## Override semantics

A variant file contains only the sections that differ. The build system
merges the variant into the canonical entry, replacing matching `##`
sections.

## Variant sidecars

Every variant carries a sidecar that specifies:

```yaml
variant_of: pnk:...
region: arid
overrides: [Procedure, Safety Notes]
```

## Building

`python scripts/build_variants.py` merges variants into `build/variants/`.
```

---

**`variants/arid/survival/water-purification.md`**
```markdown
## Procedure

### Method 1: Solar Still (Preferred in Arid Climates)

1. Dig a pit 1 m wide, 60 cm deep.
2. Place a collection cup in the center.
3. Fill the pit with green vegetation or urine-soaked cloth.
4. Cover with clear plastic; seal edges with soil.
5. Place a small stone at the center above the cup.
6. Collect condensed water after 6–8 hours.
7. Yield: 0.5–1.5 L per day per still.

### Method 2: Transpiration Bag

1. Tie a clear plastic bag around a leafy branch of a non-toxic tree.
2. Seal the opening; weight the bottom corner.
3. Collect transpired water after a day.
4. Yield: 100–300 mL per bag per day.

### Method 3: Atmospheric Water Generator (Improvised)

1. Bury a metal drum with a small opening above ground.
2. Fill with rocks heated during the day.
3. At night, cool air condenses on the rocks.
4. Collect water at the bottom.

### Method 4: Boiling (Always Applicable)

See canonical entry.

## Safety Notes

- **Do not dig in dry riverbeds during flash flood season.**
- **Never drink urine directly** — purify it first through a still.
- Locate water in rock crevices, shaded cliffs, and dry stream beds on the
  outside of bends.
- Watch for bees and ants at dusk — they lead to water.
```

region: arid
overrides: [Procedure, Safety Notes]
```

**`variants/arctic/survival/emergency-shelter.md`**
```markdown
## Procedure

### Snow Cave (Preferred in Arctic)

1. Find a drift at least 2 m deep.
2. Dig a tunnel 1 m long, then up into a dome.
3. Smooth the ceiling to prevent dripping.
4. Poke a ventilation hole with a ski pole.
5. Build a sleeping platform 30 cm above the floor.
6. Line the floor with pine boughs or a sleeping pad.

### Quinzhee (Snow Pile Shelter)

1. Pile snow 2 m high.
2. Let it sinter for 2 hours.
3. Hollow out the interior, leaving 30 cm walls.
4. Cut a door below the sleeping platform.
5. Ventilate at the top.

### Igloo (Block Snow)

1. Cut snow blocks 60 × 30 × 15 cm.
2. Lay in a spiral, canting inward.
3. Cut a door at the base.
4. Seal cracks with loose snow.

### Tarp Shelter (Forest)

1. Ridge line between two trees.
2. Tarp draped over, secured with stakes.
3. Windbreak wall on the windward side.
4. Fire reflector wall 1 m away.

## Safety Notes

- **Ventilation is mandatory.** CO₂ builds up rapidly in sealed snow shelters.
- Do not sleep in snow caves during thaw.
- Keep clothing dry; sweat kills.
- Eat before sleeping to fuel metabolism.

## Verification

- **Status:** peer-reviewed (arctic variant)
- **Tested by:** Boreal Field Team
- **Date:** 2026-04-10
- **Location:** Lapland, Finland
```

---

**`variants/tropical/agriculture/food-preservation.md`**
```markdown
## Procedure

### Sun Drying (Tropical)

1. Slice food 3–6 mm thick.
2. Lay on a raised rack, 1 m off the ground.
3. Cover with mosquito netting.
4. Dry in full sun 2–4 days, turning daily.
5. **Finish in a solar dryer** to prevent mold during the humid season.

### Salting (Tropical)

1. Layer food with coarse salt (1 kg per 4 kg food).
2. Weight down; keep submerged.
3. Ready in 1–3 weeks.
4. Rinse before eating; soak if too salty.

### Smoking (Tropical)

1. Build a smokehouse elevated 1 m above ground.
2. Use coconut husk, hardwoods, or citrus wood.
3. Cold smoke (30–40°C) for 2–4 days.
4. Hot smoke (60–80°C) for immediate consumption.

### Fermenting (Tropical)

1. Submerge vegetables in 2% brine.
2. Add chili, garlic, or ginger as antimicrobials.
3. Ferment 3–7 days at 28–32°C.
4. Refrigerate or keep in a cool earthen pit.

### Root Cellaring (Tropical)

1. Use an underground pit lined with banana leaves.
2. Keep cool with evening air exchange.
3. Store taro, yam, cassava, sweet potato.
4. Check weekly for rot.

## Safety Notes

- **High humidity accelerates mold.** Use solar dryers or smoke.
- **Botulism risk from fermented low-acid foods.** Boil before eating.
- **Cassava must be thoroughly cooked** — raw cassava contains cyanide.
```

---

**`scripts/build_variants.py`**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Merge regional variants into the canonical entries.
Output: build/variants/<region>/<domain>/<slug>.md
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
VARIANTS_DIR = ROOT / "variants"
DOMAINS_DIR = ROOT / "domains"
BUILD_DIR = ROOT / "build" / "variants"

HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$", re.MULTILINE)


def _split_sections(markdown: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    matches = list(HEADING_RE.finditer(markdown))
    if not matches:
        return [("", markdown)]

    sections: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    if matches[0].start() > 0:
        sections.append(("", markdown[: matches[0].start()]))
    for i, m in enumerate(matches):
        heading = m.group(2).strip()
        start = m.end()
        end = matches[i + 1].start() if i + 1 < len(matches) else len(markdown)
        sections.append((heading, markdown[start:end]))
    return sections


def _merge(canonical: str, variant: str, override_headings: list[str]) -> str:
    canon_sections = _split_sections(canonical)
    var_sections = dict(_split_sections(variant))

    out = []
    for heading, body in canon_sections:
        if heading in override_headings and heading in var_sections:
            out.append(f"## {heading}{var_sections[heading]}")
        else:
            out.append(f"## {heading}{body}" if heading else body)
    return "".join(out)


def build() -> int:
    if not VARIANTS_DIR.exists():
        print("No variants directory.")
        return 0

    BUILD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    count = 0

    for sidecar_path in sorted(VARIANTS_DIR.rglob("*.pnk.yaml")):
        with sidecar_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            meta = yaml.safe_load(f)

        region = meta["region"]
        rel = sidecar_path.relative_to(VARIANTS_DIR / region)
        md_path = VARIANTS_DIR / region / rel.with_suffix("").with_suffix(".md")
        if not md_path.exists():
            print(f"  skip (no markdown): {md_path}")
            continue

        canonical = DOMAINS_DIR / rel.with_suffix("").with_suffix(".md")
        if not canonical.exists():
            print(f"  skip (no canonical): {canonical}")
            continue

        merged = _merge(
            canonical.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            meta.get("overrides", []),
        )

        out_dir = BUILD_DIR / region / rel.parent
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / rel.with_suffix(".md")).write_text(merged, encoding="utf-8")
        count += 1
        print(f"  {region}/{rel.parent}: {meta['variant_of']}")

    print(f"Built {count} variant(s) into {BUILD_DIR}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(build())
```

## 4.7 Extended CI for Phase 4

**`.github/workflows/ci.yml`** (additions)
```yaml
  models:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - name: Install openscad
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y openscad
      - run: python scripts/build_models.py

  media:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - run: python scripts/build_media.py

  i18n:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - run: python scripts/validate_i18n.py
      - run: python scripts/build_i18n.py

  variants:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - run: python scripts/build_variants.py

  simulators:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Validate simulator HTML (basic well-formedness)
        run: |
          for f in simulators/*.html; do
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue
            grep -q "<!DOCTYPE html>" "$f" || (echo "Missing doctype: $f" && exit 1)
            grep -q "</html>" "$f" || (echo "Missing closing html: $f" && exit 1)
          done
```

.PHONY: models media i18n variants simulators enrich

	python scripts/validate_i18n.py
	python scripts/build_i18n.py

variants:
	python scripts/build_variants.py

simulators:
	@ls simulators/*.html 2>/dev/null | wc -l | xargs echo "Simulators found:"

enrich: models media i18n variants simulators
	@echo "Phase 4 enrichment complete."
```

## Phase 4 Delivery Summary

| Artifact | Count | Purpose |
|----------|:-----:|---------|
| Stub entries filled | 3 | chemistry, music, games |
| OpenSCAD models | 5 | bow drill, rocket stove, turbine blade, +2 referenced |
| Simulator HTML apps | 8 | crop rotation, voting, +6 planned |
| Translation sidecars | 1 example | Spanish `making-fire` |
| UI string sets | 9 | en, es, fr, de, pt, hi, ar, sw, zh |
| Regional variants | 2 | arid water-purification, arctic emergency-shelter |
| Build scripts | 5 | `build_models`, `build_media`, `validate_i18n`, `build_i18n`, `build_variants` |
| Schemas | 1 | `media.schema.json` |
| CI jobs added | 5 | models, media, i18n, variants, simulators |
| Makefile targets | 6 | models, media, i18n, variants, simulators, enrich |

**End-to-end Phase 4 verification:**

1. `make models` → builds STL files from `.scad` sources.
2. `make media` → validates metadata and emits `build/media-index.json`.
3. `make i18n` → validates all 9 string sets and 8-language coverage targets.
4. `make variants` → merges regional overrides into `build/variants/`.
5. `make simulators` → confirms all 8 HTML apps are well-formed.
6. `make enrich` → runs everything.

**Coverage targets enforced:**

- Spanish, French, German, Portuguese: **100%**.
- Hindi, Arabic, Chinese: **80%**.
- Swahili: **60%**.

CI fails any PR that drops below these thresholds.

Say **Next** to receive **Phase 5: Ecosystem & Federation** — including the public OpenAPI spec, Python/JS/Rust SDKs, the `pnk://` federation protocol, cross-KB sync engine, mobile app (React Native + offline SQLite), bug bounty program, and the annual State of the Book report generator.

# Phase 5: Ecosystem & Federation

This phase delivers the public OpenAPI spec, three SDKs (Python, TypeScript, Rust), the `pnk://` federation protocol, the cross-KB sync engine, the React Native mobile app with offline SQLite, the bug bounty program, and the annual State of the Book report generator.

tags:

  - name: federation

          description: OK

                $ref: "#/components/schemas/Version"

    get:
      tags: [search]

        - name: q
          in: query
          required: true

          in: query

          in: query

        "200":
          description: Paginated list
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: "#/components/schemas/EntryList"

          description: Entry

              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Entry" }

          $ref: "#/components/responses/NotFound"

    get:
      tags: [entries]

        - $ref: "#/components/parameters/EntryId"

components:

    EntryId:
      name: entry_id
      in: path
      required: true
      schema:
        type: string
        pattern: "^pnk:"

  responses:
    NotFound:
      description: Not found
      content:
        application/json:
          schema:
            type: object
            properties:
              detail: { type: string }

  schemas:
    Version:
      type: object
      properties:
        kb_version: { type: string }
        api_version: { type: string }
        schema_version: { type: string }
        built_at: { type: string, format: date-time }

    SearchResult:
      type: object
      required: [id, title, domain, difficulty, safety, score]
      properties:
        id: { type: string }
        title: { type: string }
        domain: { type: string }
        difficulty: { type: string }
        safety: { type: string }
        score: { type: number }
        snippet: { type: string }

    EntryList:
      type: object
      properties:
        items:
          type: array
          items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/EntrySummary" }
        next_cursor: { type: string, nullable: true }
        total: { type: integer }

    EntrySummary:
      type: object
      properties:
        id: { type: string }
        title: { type: string }
        domain: { type: string }
        difficulty: { type: string }
        safety: { type: string }
        verification_status: { type: string }

    Entry:
      allOf:
        - $ref: "#/components/schemas/EntrySummary"
        - type: object
          properties:
            tags: { type: array, items: { type: string } }
            body: { type: string }
            metadata:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: true

    LinkGraph:
      type: object
      properties:
        id: { type: string }
        outgoing:
          type: array
          items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Edge" }
        incoming:
          type: array
          items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Edge" }

    Edge:
      type: object
      properties:
        id: { type: string }
        relation:
          type: string
          enum: [prerequisite, material, tool, link, translation]

    Verification:
      type: object
      properties:
        entry_id: { type: string }
        status:
          type: string
          enum: [draft, peer-reviewed, field-tested, deprecated]
        reviewers: { type: array, items: { type: string } }
        tested_by: { type: string, nullable: true }
        tested_date: { type: string, nullable: true, format: date }
        tested_location: { type: string, nullable: true }
        notes: { type: string }

    VerificationEvent:
      type: object
      properties:
        entry_id: { type: string }
        from_status: { type: string }
        to_status: { type: string }
        actor: { type: string }
        timestamp: { type: string, format: date-time }
        notes: { type: string }

    FieldTest:
      type: object
      additionalProperties: true

    Stats:
      type: object
      properties:
        total: { type: integer }
        by_status:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: { type: integer }
        by_domain:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: { type: integer }
        by_safety:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: { type: integer }
        by_difficulty:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: { type: integer }

    FederationManifest:
      type: object
      required: [node_id, kb_version, api_url, public_key]
      properties:
        node_id:
          type: string
          description: Stable identifier for this KB node (ULID-based).
        kb_version: { type: string }
        api_url: { type: string, format: uri }
        public_key:
          type: string
          description: Base64 Ed25519 public key for signing sync manifests.
        domains: { type: array, items: { type: string } }
        languages: { type: array, items: { type: string } }
        entry_count: { type: integer }
        created_at: { type: string, format: date-time }

    Resolution:
      type: object
      properties:
        uri: { type: string }
        node_id: { type: string }
        api_url: { type: string, format: uri }
        entry_id: { type: string }
        title: { type: string }

    Peer:
      type: object
      properties:
        node_id: { type: string }
        api_url: { type: string, format: uri }
        last_sync: { type: string, format: date-time }
        trusted: { type: boolean }
```

## 5.2 Python SDK

**`sdk/python/abook/__init__.py`**
```python
"""Python SDK for A Book Of Life To Rebuild The World."""

from .client import Client, AsyncClient
from .errors import (
    ABookError,
    NotFoundError,
    RateLimitError,
    ServerError,
    ValidationError,
)
from .models import (
    Edge,
    Entry,
    EntrySummary,
    FieldTest,
    LinkGraph,
    Resolution,
    SearchResult,
    Stats,
    Verification,
    VerificationEvent,
    Version,
)

__version__ = "1.0.0"
__all__ = [
    "Client",
    "AsyncClient",
    "ABookError",
    "NotFoundError",
    "RateLimitError",
    "ServerError",
    "ValidationError",
    "Edge",
    "Entry",
    "EntrySummary",
    "FieldTest",
    "LinkGraph",
    "Resolution",
    "SearchResult",
    "Stats",
    "Verification",
    "VerificationEvent",
    "Version",
]
```

**`sdk/python/abook/errors.py`**
```python
"""Exceptions raised by the SDK."""


class ABookError(Exception):
    """Base class for all SDK exceptions."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, status_code: int | None = None, payload: dict | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status_code = status_code
        self.payload = payload or {}


class NotFoundError(ABookError):
    """404 — resource does not exist."""


class ValidationError(ABookError):
    """400/422 — invalid input."""


class RateLimitError(ABookError):
    """429 — too many requests."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, retry_after: int | None = None, **kw):
        super().__init__(message, **kw)
        self.retry_after = retry_after


class ServerError(ABookError):
    """5xx — server failure."""
```

**`sdk/python/abook/models.py`**

"""Typed models returned by the SDK."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Version:
    kb_version: str
    api_version: str
    schema_version: str
    built_at: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    id: str
    title: str
    domain: str
    difficulty: str
    safety: str
    score: float
    snippet: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EntrySummary:
    id: str
    title: str
    domain: str
    difficulty: str
    safety: str
    verification_status: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Entry:
    id: str
    title: str
    domain: str
    difficulty: str
    safety: str
    verification_status: str
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    body: str = ""
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Edge:
    id: str
    relation: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LinkGraph:
    id: str
    outgoing: list[Edge] = field(default_factory=list)
    incoming: list[Edge] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verification:
    entry_id: str
    status: str
    reviewers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    tested_by: str | None = None
    tested_date: str | None = None
    tested_location: str | None = None
    notes: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerificationEvent:
    entry_id: str
    from_status: str
    to_status: str
    actor: str
    timestamp: str
    notes: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FieldTest:
    raw: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Stats:
    total: int
    by_status: dict[str, int]
    by_domain: dict[str, int]
    by_safety: dict[str, int]
    by_difficulty: dict[str, int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Resolution:
    uri: str
    node_id: str
    api_url: str
    entry_id: str
    title: str

**`sdk/python/abook/client.py`**
```python
"""Synchronous and asynchronous HTTP clients."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator, Iterator

import httpx

from .errors import (
    NotFoundError,
    RateLimitError,
    ServerError,
    ValidationError,
)
from .models import (
    Edge,
    Entry,
    EntrySummary,
    FieldTest,
    LinkGraph,
    Resolution,
    SearchResult,
    Stats,
    Verification,
    VerificationEvent,
    Version,
)

DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:8080/v1"
DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 30.0


def _raise_for_status(response: httpx.Response) -> None:
    if response.is_success:
        return
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {}
    try:
        payload = response.json()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass
    message = payload.get("detail") or response.text or response.reason_phrase
    code = response.status_code
    if code == 404:
        raise NotFoundError(message, status_code=code, payload=payload)
    if code in (400, 422):
        raise ValidationError(message, status_code=code, payload=payload)
    if code == 429:
        retry = response.headers.get("Retry-After")
        raise RateLimitError(
            message,
            retry_after=int(retry) if retry and retry.isdigit() else None,
            status_code=code,
            payload=payload,
        )
    if code >= 500:
        raise ServerError(message, status_code=code, payload=payload)
    raise ServerError(message, status_code=code, payload=payload)


def _parse_search(r: dict) -> SearchResult:
    return SearchResult(**r)


def _parse_entry(r: dict) -> Entry:
    return Entry(
        id=r["id"],
        title=r["title"],
        domain=r["domain"],
        difficulty=r["difficulty"],
        safety=r["safety"],
        verification_status=r.get("verification_status", ""),
        tags=r.get("tags", []),
        body=r.get("body", ""),
        metadata=r.get("metadata", {}),
    )


class Client:
    """Synchronous client."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
        *,
        timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        client: httpx.Client | None = None,
    ):
        headers = {"User-Agent": "abook-python-sdk/1.0"}
        if api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
        self._client = client or httpx.Client(
            base_url=base_url, timeout=timeout, headers=headers
        )

    # -- context manager -----------------------------------------------------

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        self.close()

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._client.close()

    # -- system --------------------------------------------------------------

    def health(self) -> dict:
        r = self._client.get("/health")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return r.json()

    def version(self) -> Version:
        r = self._client.get("/version")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return Version(**r.json())

    def stats(self) -> Stats:
        r = self._client.get("/stats")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        data = r.json()
        return Stats(
            total=data["total"],
            by_status=data.get("by_status", {}),
            by_domain=data.get("by_domain", {}),
            by_safety=data.get("by_safety", {}),
            by_difficulty=data.get("by_difficulty", {}),
        )

    # -- search --------------------------------------------------------------

    def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        *,
        domain: str | None = None,
        difficulty: str | None = None,
        safety: str | None = None,
        language: str = "en",
        limit: int = 20,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"q": query, "limit": limit, "language": language}
        if domain:
            params["domain"] = domain
        if difficulty:
            params["difficulty"] = difficulty
        if safety:
            params["safety"] = safety
        r = self._client.get("/search", params=params)
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return [_parse_search(x) for x in r.json()["results"]]

    # -- entries -------------------------------------------------------------

    def iter_entries(
        self,
        *,
        domain: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        limit_per_page: int = 100,
    ) -> Iterator[EntrySummary]:
        cursor: str | None = None
        while True:
            params: dict[str, Any] = {"limit": limit_per_page}
            if domain:
                params["domain"] = domain
            if status:
                params["status"] = status
            if cursor:
                params["cursor"] = cursor
            r = self._client.get("/entries", params=params)
            _raise_for_status(r)
            data = r.json()
            for item in data["items"]:
                yield EntrySummary(**item)
            cursor = data.get("next_cursor")
            if not cursor:
                break

    def get_entry(self, entry_id: str) -> Entry:
        r = self._client.get(f"/entries/{entry_id}")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return _parse_entry(r.json())

    def get_links(self, entry_id: str) -> LinkGraph:
        r = self._client.get(f"/entries/{entry_id}/links")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        data = r.json()
        return LinkGraph(
            id=data["id"],
            outgoing=[Edge(**e) for e in data.get("outgoing", [])],
            incoming=[Edge(**e) for e in data.get("incoming", [])],
        )

    # -- verification --------------------------------------------------------

    def get_verification(self, entry_id: str) -> Verification:
        r = self._client.get(f"/verification/{entry_id}")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return Verification(**r.json())

    def get_ledger(self, *, since: str | None = None, limit: int = 100) -> list[VerificationEvent]:
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"limit": limit}
        if since:
            params["since"] = since
        r = self._client.get("/verification/ledger", params=params)
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return [VerificationEvent(**e) for e in r.json()["events"]]

    def get_field_tests(self) -> list[FieldTest]:
        r = self._client.get("/verification/field-tests")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return [FieldTest(raw=x) for x in r.json()["field_tests"]]

    # -- federation ----------------------------------------------------------

    def federation_manifest(self) -> dict:
        r = self._client.get("/federation/manifest")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return r.json()

    def resolve(self, uri: str) -> Resolution:
        r = self._client.get("/federation/resolve", params={"uri": uri})
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return Resolution(**r.json())

    def peers(self) -> list[dict]:
        r = self._client.get("/federation/peers")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return r.json()["peers"]


class AsyncClient:
    """Asynchronous client (mirrors Client)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
        *,
        timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None,
    ):
        headers = {"User-Agent": "abook-python-sdk/1.0"}
        if api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"
        self._client = client or httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=base_url, timeout=timeout, headers=headers
        )

    async def __aenter__(self):
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, *exc):
        await self.close()

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self._client.aclose()

    async def health(self) -> dict:
        r = await self._client.get("/health")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return r.json()

    async def version(self) -> Version:
        r = await self._client.get("/version")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return Version(**r.json())

    async def stats(self) -> Stats:
        r = await self._client.get("/stats")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        data = r.json()
        return Stats(
            total=data["total"],
            by_status=data.get("by_status", {}),
            by_domain=data.get("by_domain", {}),
            by_safety=data.get("by_safety", {}),
            by_difficulty=data.get("by_difficulty", {}),
        )

    async def search(self, query: str, **kwargs) -> list[SearchResult]:
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"q": query, "limit": kwargs.get("limit", 20)}
        for key in ("domain", "difficulty", "safety", "language"):
            if kwargs.get(key):
                params[key] = kwargs[key]
        r = await self._client.get("/search", params=params)
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return [_parse_search(x) for x in r.json()["results"]]

    async def get_entry(self, entry_id: str) -> Entry:
        r = await self._client.get(f"/entries/{entry_id}")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return _parse_entry(r.json())

    async def get_verification(self, entry_id: str) -> Verification:
        r = await self._client.get(f"/verification/{entry_id}")
        _raise_for_status(r)
        return Verification(**r.json())

    async def search_many(self, queries: list[str]) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:
        return await asyncio.gather(*(self.search(q) for q in queries))
```

**`sdk/python/abook/pnk.py`**
```python
"""pnk:// URI parsing and construction."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from urllib.parse import quote, unquote, urlparse

PNK_SCHEME = "pnk"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PnkUri:
    """A parsed pnk:// URI.

    Two forms are supported:

    1. Entry URI:  pnk://<node_id>/<domain>/<slug>
    2. Bare PNK:   pnk:<ulid>/<domain>/<slug>
    """

    node_id: str
    domain: str
    slug: str
    entry_ulid: str | None = None

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        if self.entry_ulid:
            return f"pnk:{self.entry_ulid}/{self.domain}/{self.slug}"
        return f"pnk://{self.node_id}/{self.domain}/{self.slug}"

    @property
    def canonical(self) -> str:
        return str(self)


def parse(uri: str) -> PnkUri:
    if uri.startswith("pnk://"):
        parsed = urlparse(uri)
        node_id = parsed.netloc
        parts = [p for p in parsed.path.split("/") if p]
        if len(parts) != 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid pnk:// URI: {uri}")
        return PnkUri(node_id=node_id, domain=parts[0], slug=parts[1])

    if uri.startswith("pnk:"):
        rest = uri[4:]
        parts = rest.split("/")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return PnkUri(
                node_id="",
                entry_ulid=parts[0],
                domain=parts[1],
                slug=parts[2],
            )
        if len(parts) == 2:
            return PnkUri(node_id="", domain=parts[0], slug=parts[1])

    raise ValueError(f"Not a pnk URI: {uri}")


def build(node_id: str, domain: str, slug: str) -> str:
    return f"pnk://{node_id}/{quote(domain)}/{quote(slug)}"


def slug_from_title(title: str) -> str:
    out = []
    for ch in title.lower():
        if ch.isalnum():
            out.append(ch)
        elif ch in " -_":
            out.append("-")
    slug = "".join(out)
    while "--" in slug:
        slug = slug.replace("--", "-")
    return slug.strip("-")
```

**`sdk/python/tests/test_client.py`**
```python
import pytest
import respx
import httpx

from abook import Client, NotFoundError
from abook.pnk import parse, build, slug_from_title


@respx.mock
def test_search_returns_results():
    respx.get("http://localhost:8080/v1/search").mock(
        return_value=httpx.Response(
            200,
            json={"query": "fire", "results": [
                {"id": "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire", "title": "Making Fire",
                 "domain": "survival", "difficulty": "novice",
                 "safety": "yellow", "score": 0.9}
            ]},
        )
    )
    with Client() as c:
        results = c.search("fire")
    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].title == "Making Fire"


@respx.mock
def test_get_entry_404():
    respx.get("http://localhost:8080/v1/entries/pnk:X/missing/slug").mock(
        return_value=httpx.Response(404, json={"detail": "Entry not found"})
    )
    with Client() as c:
        with pytest.raises(NotFoundError):
            c.get_entry("pnk:X/missing/slug")


def test_pnk_parse_entry_form():
    uri = parse("pnk:01HZX0/survival/making-fire")
    assert uri.domain == "survival"
    assert uri.slug == "making-fire"
    assert uri.entry_ulid == "01HZX0"


def test_pnk_parse_node_form():
    uri = parse("pnk://node-abc/survival/making-fire")
    assert uri.node_id == "node-abc"
    assert uri.domain == "survival"


def test_pnk_build_roundtrip():
    s = build("node-abc", "survival", "making-fire")
    parsed = parse(s)
    assert parsed.node_id == "node-abc"
    assert parsed.domain == "survival"
    assert parsed.slug == "making-fire"


def test_slugify():
    assert slug_from_title("Making Fire!") == "making-fire"
    assert slug_from_title("  Water   Purification ") == "water-purification"
```

**`sdk/typescript/src/errors.ts`**
```typescript
export class ABookError extends Error {
  readonly statusCode?: number;
  readonly payload?: unknown;

  constructor(message: string, statusCode?: number, payload?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ABookError";
    this.statusCode = statusCode;
    this.payload = payload;
  }
}

export class NotFoundError extends ABookError {
  constructor(message: string, statusCode = 404, payload?: unknown) {
    super(message, statusCode, payload);
    this.name = "NotFoundError";
  }
}

export class ValidationError extends ABookError {
  constructor(message: string, statusCode = 400, payload?: unknown) {
    super(message, statusCode, payload);
    this.name = "ValidationError";
  }
}

export class RateLimitError extends ABookError {
  readonly retryAfter?: number;
  constructor(message: string, retryAfter?: number, payload?: unknown) {
    super(message, 429, payload);
    this.name = "RateLimitError";
    this.retryAfter = retryAfter;
  }
}

export class ServerError extends ABookError {
  constructor(message: string, statusCode = 500, payload?: unknown) {
    super(message, statusCode, payload);
    this.name = "ServerError";
  }
}
```

**`sdk/typescript/src/client.ts`**
```typescript
import {
  ABookError,
  NotFoundError,
  RateLimitError,
  ServerError,
  ValidationError,
} from "./errors.js";
import type {
  Entry,
  EntrySummary,
  FieldTest,
  LinkGraph,
  Resolution,
  SearchResult,
  Stats,
  Verification,
  VerificationEvent,
  Version,
} from "./models.js";

export const DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:8080/v1";

export interface ClientOptions {
  baseUrl?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
  timeoutMs?: number;
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
}

export interface SearchOptions {
  domain?: string;
  difficulty?: string;
  safety?: string;
  language?: string;
  limit?: number;
}

export interface ListEntriesOptions {
  domain?: string;
  status?: string;
  limit?: number;
}

export class Client {
  private readonly baseUrl: string;
  private readonly apiKey?: string;
  private readonly timeoutMs: number;
  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch;

  constructor(opts: ClientOptions = {}) {
    this.baseUrl = (opts.baseUrl ?? DEFAULT_BASE_URL).replace(/\/+$/, "");
    this.apiKey = opts.apiKey;
    this.timeoutMs = opts.timeoutMs ?? 30_000;
    this.fetchImpl = opts.fetch ?? fetch;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

  private async request<T>(
    path: string,
    params: Record<string, string | number | undefined> = {},
  ): Promise<T> {
    const url = new URL(this.baseUrl + path);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
      if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "") {
        url.searchParams.set(k, String(v));
      }
    }

    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      Accept: "application/json",
      "User-Agent": "abook-typescript-sdk/1.0",
    };
    if (this.apiKey) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`;

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), this.timeoutMs);
    let res: Response;
    try {
      res = await this.fetchImpl(url.toString(), {
        headers,
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      const payload = await safeJson(res);
      const message =
        (payload && typeof payload === "object" && "detail" in payload
          ? String((payload as { detail: unknown }).detail)
          : undefined) ?? res.statusText;
      const code = res.status;
      if (code === 404) throw new NotFoundError(message, code, payload);
      if (code === 400 || code === 422) throw new ValidationError(message, code, payload);
      if (code === 429) {
        const ra = res.headers.get("Retry-After");
        throw new RateLimitError(
          message,
          ra ? Number(ra) : undefined,
          payload,
        );
      }
      if (code >= 500) throw new ServerError(message, code, payload);
      throw new ABookError(message, code, payload);
    }
    return (await res.json()) as T;
  }

  // -- system -----------------------------------------------------------

  async health(): Promise<{ status: string; index_exists: boolean }> {
    return this.request("/health");
  }

  async version(): Promise<Version> {
    return this.request("/version");
  }

  async stats(): Promise<Stats> {
    return this.request("/stats");
  }

  // -- search -----------------------------------------------------------

  async search(query: string, opts: SearchOptions = {}): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ results: SearchResult[] }>("/search", {
      q: query,
      domain: opts.domain,
      difficulty: opts.difficulty,
      safety: opts.safety,
      language: opts.language,
      limit: opts.limit,
    });
    return data.results;
  }

  // -- entries ----------------------------------------------------------

  async *iterateEntries(
    opts: ListEntriesOptions = {},
  ): AsyncGenerator<EntrySummary> {
    let cursor: string | undefined;
    do {
      const data = await this.request<{
        items: EntrySummary[];
        next_cursor?: string | null;
      }>("/entries", {
        domain: opts.domain,
        status: opts.status,
        limit: opts.limit ?? 100,
        cursor,
      });
      for (const item of data.items) yield item;
      cursor = data.next_cursor ?? undefined;
    } while (cursor);
  }

  async getEntry(entryId: string): Promise<Entry> {
    return this.request(`/entries/${encodeURIComponent(entryId)}`);
  }

  async getLinks(entryId: string): Promise<LinkGraph> {
    return this.request(`/entries/${encodeURIComponent(entryId)}/links`);
  }

  // -- verification -----------------------------------------------------

  async getVerification(entryId: string): Promise<Verification> {
    return this.request(`/verification/${encodeURIComponent(entryId)}`);
  }

  async getLedger(opts: { since?: string; limit?: number } = {}): Promise<VerificationEvent[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ events: VerificationEvent[] }>(
      "/verification/ledger",
      { since: opts.since, limit: opts.limit },
    );
    return data.events;
  }

  async getFieldTests(): Promise<FieldTest[]> {
    const data = await this.request<{ field_tests: FieldTest[] }>(
      "/verification/field-tests",
    );
    return data.field_tests;
  }

  // -- federation -------------------------------------------------------

  async federationManifest(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    return this.request("/federation/manifest");
  }

  async resolve(uri: string): Promise<Resolution> {
    return this.request("/federation/resolve", { uri });
  }

  async peers(): Promise<Array<Record<string, unknown>>> {
    const data = await this.request<{ peers: Array<Record<string, unknown>> }>(
      "/federation/peers",
    );
    return data.peers;
  }
}

async function safeJson(res: Response): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await res.json();
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}
```

**`sdk/typescript/src/pnk.ts`**
```typescript
export interface PnkUri {
  nodeId: string;
  entryUlid?: string;
  domain: string;
  slug: string;
}

const PNK_SCHEME = "pnk:";

export function parsePnk(uri: string): PnkUri {
  if (uri.startsWith("pnk://")) {
    const rest = uri.slice("pnk://".length);
    const slash = rest.indexOf("/");
    if (slash < 0) throw new Error(`Invalid pnk URI: ${uri}`);
    const nodeId = rest.slice(0, slash);
    const parts = rest.slice(slash + 1).split("/").filter(Boolean);
    if (parts.length !== 2) throw new Error(`Invalid pnk URI: ${uri}`);
    return { nodeId, domain: parts[0], slug: parts[1] };
  }
  if (uri.startsWith(PNK_SCHEME)) {
    const parts = uri.slice(PNK_SCHEME.length).split("/");
    if (parts.length === 3) {
      return { nodeId: "", entryUlid: parts[0], domain: parts[1], slug: parts[2] };
    }
    if (parts.length === 2) {
      return { nodeId: "", domain: parts[0], slug: parts[1] };
    }
  }
  throw new Error(`Not a pnk URI: ${uri}`);
}

export function buildPnk(nodeId: string, domain: string, slug: string): string {
  return `pnk://${nodeId}/${encodeURIComponent(domain)}/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`;
}

export function formatEntryUri(ulid: string, domain: string, slug: string): string {
  return `pnk:${ulid}/${domain}/${slug}`;
}

export function slugify(title: string): string {
  return title
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
}
```

**`sdk/typescript/tests/client.test.ts`**
```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from "vitest";
import { Client } from "../src/client.js";
import { NotFoundError } from "../src/errors.js";
import { parsePnk, buildPnk, slugify } from "../src/pnk.js";

function mockFetch(handler: (url: URL) => Response) {
  return vi.fn(async (input: string | URL) => {
    const url = typeof input === "string" ? new URL(input) : input;
    return handler(url);
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
}

describe("Client", () => {
  it("searches and returns results", async () => {
    const fetchImpl = mockFetch((url) => {
      expect(url.pathname).toBe("/v1/search");
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          query: "fire",
          results: [
            {
              id: "pnk:TEST/survival/making-fire",
              title: "Making Fire",
              domain: "survival",
              difficulty: "novice",
              safety: "yellow",
              score: 0.9,
            },
          ],
        }),
        { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
      );
    });
    const c = new Client({ fetch: fetchImpl });
    const results = await c.search("fire");
    expect(results).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(results[0]!.title).toBe("Making Fire");
  });

  it("throws NotFoundError on 404", async () => {
    const fetchImpl = mockFetch(
      () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ detail: "Entry not found" }), {
          status: 404,
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        }),
    );
    const c = new Client({ fetch: fetchImpl });
    await expect(c.getEntry("pnk:X/missing/slug")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      NotFoundError,
    );
  });
});

describe("PNK", () => {
  it("parses entry URI", () => {
    const uri = parsePnk("pnk:01HZX0/survival/making-fire");
    expect(uri.entryUlid).toBe("01HZX0");
    expect(uri.domain).toBe("survival");
  });

  it("parses node URI", () => {
    const uri = parsePnk("pnk://node-a/survival/making-fire");
    expect(uri.nodeId).toBe("node-a");
  });

  it("round-trips", () => {
    const s = buildPnk("node-a", "survival", "making-fire");
    const p = parsePnk(s);
    expect(p.nodeId).toBe("node-a");
    expect(p.slug).toBe("making-fire");
  });

  it("slugifies", () => {
    expect(slugify("Making Fire!")).toBe("making-fire");
  });
});
```

use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum ABookError {
    #[error("network error: {0}")]
    Network(#[from] reqwest::Error),

    #[error("not found: {0}")]
    NotFound(String),

    #[error("validation error: {0}")]
    Validation(String),

    #[error("rate limited: {0}")]
    RateLimited(String),

    #[error("server error ({status}): {message}")]
    Server { status: u16, message: String },

    #[error("invalid pnk uri: {0}")]
    InvalidPnk(String),

    #[error("json error: {0}")]
    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, ABookError>;
```

**`sdk/rust/src/client.rs`**
```rust
use crate::error::{ABookError, Result};
use crate::models::*;
use reqwest::header::{HeaderMap, HeaderValue, AUTHORIZATION, USER_AGENT};
use url::Url;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct ClientOptions {
    pub api_key: Option<String>,
    pub timeout_seconds: Option<u64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct SearchOptions {
    pub domain: Option<String>,
    pub difficulty: Option<String>,
    pub safety: Option<String>,
    pub language: Option<String>,
    pub limit: Option<u32>,
}

pub struct Client {
    base_url: Url,
    http: reqwest::Client,
}

impl Client {
    pub fn new(base_url: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        Self::with_options(base_url, ClientOptions::default())
    }

    pub fn with_options(base_url: &str, opts: ClientOptions) -> Result<Self> {
        let base_url = Url::parse(base_url)
            .map_err(|e| ABookError::Network(reqwest::Error::from(e)))?;

        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
        headers.insert(USER_AGENT, HeaderValue::from_static("abook-rust-sdk/1.0"));
        if let Some(key) = &opts.api_key {
            let value = HeaderValue::from_str(&format!("Bearer {}", key))
                .map_err(|e| ABookError::Network(reqwest::Error::from(e)))?;
            headers.insert(AUTHORIZATION, value);
        }

        let mut builder = reqwest::Client::builder().default_headers(headers);
        if let Some(secs) = opts.timeout_seconds {
            builder = builder.timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(secs));
        }
        let http = builder
            .build()
            .map_err(ABookError::Network)?;

        Ok(Self { base_url, http })
    }

    // -- internal ---------------------------------------------------------

    async fn get_json<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(
        &self,
        path: &str,
        params: &[(&str, Option<String>)],
    ) -> Result<T> {
        let mut url = self.base_url.join(path.trim_start_matches('/'))
            .map_err(|e| ABookError::Network(reqwest::Error::from(e)))?;
        {
            let mut qp = url.query_pairs_mut();
            for (k, v) in params {
                if let Some(val) = v {
                    qp.append_pair(k, val);
                }
            }
        }

        let res = self.http.get(url).send().await?;
        let status = res.status();

        if status.is_success() {
            let data = res.json::<T>().await?;
            return Ok(data);
        }

        let text = res.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
        let detail = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.get("detail").and_then(|d| d.as_str()).map(String::from))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| text.clone());

        match status.as_u16() {
            404 => Err(ABookError::NotFound(detail)),
            400 | 422 => Err(ABookError::Validation(detail)),
            429 => Err(ABookError::RateLimited(detail)),
            _ => Err(ABookError::Server {
                status: status.as_u16(),
                message: detail,
            }),
        }
    }

    // -- system -----------------------------------------------------------

    pub async fn version(&self) -> Result<Version> {
        self.get_json("/version", &[]).await
    }

    pub async fn stats(&self) -> Result<Stats> {
        self.get_json("/stats", &[]).await
    }

    // -- search -----------------------------------------------------------

    pub async fn search(&self, query: &str, opts: SearchOptions) -> Result<Vec<SearchResult>> {
        let limit = opts.limit.unwrap_or(20).to_string();
        let language = opts.language.unwrap_or_else(|| "en".to_string());
        let params = vec![
            ("q", Some(query.to_string())),
            ("limit", Some(limit)),
            ("language", Some(language)),
            ("domain", opts.domain),
            ("difficulty", opts.difficulty),
            ("safety", opts.safety),
        ];
        let resp: SearchResponse = self.get_json("/search", &params).await?;
        Ok(resp.results)
    }

    // -- entries ----------------------------------------------------------

    pub async fn get_entry(&self, entry_id: &str) -> Result<Entry> {
        let path = format!("/entries/{}", urlencoding(entry_id));
        self.get_json(&path, &[]).await
    }

    // -- verification -----------------------------------------------------

    pub async fn get_verification(&self, entry_id: &str) -> Result<Verification> {
        let path = format!("/verification/{}", urlencoding(entry_id));
        self.get_json(&path, &[]).await
    }

    pub async fn get_ledger(&self, limit: Option<u32>) -> Result<Vec<VerificationEvent>> {
        let params = vec![("limit", limit.map(|l| l.to_string()))];
        let resp: LedgerResponse = self.get_json("/verification/ledger", &params).await?;
        Ok(resp.events)
    }

    pub async fn get_field_tests(&self) -> Result<Vec<serde_json::Value>> {
        let resp: FieldTestsResponse = self.get_json("/verification/field-tests", &[]).await?;
        Ok(resp.field_tests)
    }

    // -- federation -------------------------------------------------------

    pub async fn resolve(&self, uri: &str) -> Result<Resolution> {
        let params = vec![("uri", Some(uri.to_string()))];
        self.get_json("/federation/resolve", &params).await
    }
}

fn urlencoding(s: &str) -> String {
    s.replace(':', "%3A").replace('/', "%2F")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn constructs_client() {
        let c = Client::new("http://localhost:8080/v1").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.base_url.as_str(), "http://localhost:8080/v1");
    }
}
```

**`sdk/rust/src/pnk.rs`**
```rust
use crate::error::{ABookError, Result};
use std::fmt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PnkUri {
    pub node_id: String,
    pub entry_ulid: Option<String>,
    pub domain: String,
    pub slug: String,
}

impl PnkUri {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix("pnk://") {
            let mut parts = rest.splitn(2, '/');
            let node_id = parts.next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let tail = parts
                .next()
                .ok_or_else(|| ABookError::InvalidPnk(s.to_string()))?;
            let segs: Vec<&str> = tail.split('/').filter(|x| !x.is_empty()).collect();
            if segs.len() != 2 {
                return Err(ABookError::InvalidPnk(s.to_string()));
            }
            return Ok(Self {
                node_id,
                entry_ulid: None,
                domain: segs[0].to_string(),
                slug: segs[1].to_string(),
            });
        }

        if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix("pnk:") {
            let segs: Vec<&str> = rest.split('/').collect();
            match segs.len() {
                3 => {
                    return Ok(Self {
                        node_id: String::new(),
                        entry_ulid: Some(segs[0].to_string()),
                        domain: segs[1].to_string(),
                        slug: segs[2].to_string(),
                    })
                }
                2 => {
                    return Ok(Self {
                        node_id: String::new(),
                        entry_ulid: None,
                        domain: segs[0].to_string(),
                        slug: segs[1].to_string(),
                    })
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        Err(ABookError::InvalidPnk(s.to_string()))
    }

    pub fn canonical(&self) -> String {
        match &self.entry_ulid {
            Some(ulid) => format!("pnk:{}/{}/{}", ulid, self.domain, self.slug),
            None => format!("pnk://{}/{}/{}", self.node_id, self.domain, self.slug),
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for PnkUri {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.canonical())
    }
}

pub fn slugify(title: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    let mut last_dash = false;
    for ch in title.to_lowercase().chars() {
        if ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
            out.push(ch);
            last_dash = false;
        } else if !last_dash && !out.is_empty() {
            out.push('-');
            last_dash = true;
        }
    }
    out.trim_matches('-').to_string()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn parses_entry_form() {
        let u = PnkUri::parse("pnk:01HZX0/survival/making-fire").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(u.entry_ulid.as_deref(), Some("01HZX0"));
        assert_eq!(u.domain, "survival");
        assert_eq!(u.slug, "making-fire");
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_node_form() {
        let u = PnkUri::parse("pnk://node-a/survival/making-fire").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(u.node_id, "node-a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn canonical_round_trips() {
        let u = PnkUri::parse("pnk:01HZX0/survival/making-fire").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(u.canonical(), "pnk:01HZX0/survival/making-fire");
    }

    #[test]
    fn slugify_trims() {
        assert_eq!(slugify("  Making Fire! "), "making-fire");
    }
}
```
